Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Mar 14 2015 12:09:29 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Directly depositing coal royalties vs subsidizing highways using coal royalties --- why first option is less inefficient and reduces conversion / poverty / naxalism etc
.
When coal royalties are spent to say subsidize roads, not everyone benefits equally. eg say toll to road from mubai to ahmedabad when coa royalties were not used to subsidize roads was Rs 500 for car and Rs 3000 for bus . Say coal royalties are used for roads and toll becomes zero for both. Now say car had on an average 4 passengers and bus had 60 passengers. Then benefit that car passengers got from coal royalties used to subsidize roads was Rs 125 per person, while benefit bus passenger got is Rs 3000 / 50 = Rs 60 only. And consider a much much poorer person who rarely travels out of his village. Benefit he got will be near zero.
.
The pro-subsidy will start showing indirect benefits. But that is all smoke and mirror. Take all items on which govt spends as subsidies . In all cases except food, the poor get LESS per head benefit than well off.
.
In case of direct deposit of mineral royalties , directly into accounts of citizens, all get same benefit.
.
And what is worse is that when direct deposit is removed, it gives opportunities to leaders to take bribes and give away coal mines to people like Jindal for as low as Rs 108 / tonne !! IOW, no real money comes. Only thing that comes is --- promise of cheap power. But again, even if Jindal gives power to cheap, then the person who consumes 1000 units a month will benefit more than those who consume 0 units or 100 units per month !! And Jindal isnt going to pass all the benefit to consumers anyway.
.
And how does GoI's decision that royalty should NOT be deposited uin citizens' account , but used to subsidize roads etc will increase naxalism /
.
Whether we like it or not, the naxal leaders as well as Missionaries will explain these facts to poor and will try to put poison in their minds that upper class and upper caste hindus are exploiting them by not giving them fair share of the coal's royalties to them. This enable them to increase naxalism and conversion.
..
Solution I propose is that activists should force MPs to print law-drafts that will force PM / Coal Minister to deposit the coal royalties directly into citizens account. The draft is at http://tinyurl.com/DDMRCAM
.
.
I am trying to convince activists that all activosts should send following order to MP via SMS
.
====
Dear MP , If and after majority voters from your area send this link to you via SMS , please implement this order of mine, to print Direct Deposit of Mineral Royalty into Citizens Accounts and Military draft in Gazette give at http://tinyurl.com/DDMRCAM
=================
.
And IMO activists should try to reach 45 crore voters via newspaper advt and also convince them to send this order to MP via SMS

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Mar 14 2015 16:19:28 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Why a big part of coal auction revenue numbers are fake ?
.
The coal auction numbers go from Rs 2 lakh crore and we arent even half way. The "estimates" go to Rs 10 lakh crores. But much of the money will come ONLY after mining starts. And here is a reason why mining may not start on many mines.
.
Say a miner has two mines . On first he has to pay royalty of Rs 300 / tonne and in other he has to pay royalty of Rs 2000 per tonne. Obviously, he will take coal from first mine !!! Or worse, he can take coal from second mine and show on paper that coal came from first mine , and pay only Rs 300 per tonne !!!
.
All in all , there is NO MUST USE condition with mine.Not is minimum target set to each mine.
.
So till you see real money, pls dont put much expectations.
.
Solution I propose is that mines should have been allocated to miner who gives least mining cost, all coal buyers should be given coal at same price, and transport cost of coal from any mine to any DESTINATION train station should be constant irrespective of distance between coal mine and destination train station. So the coal transportaion will get subsidies at some point and at some point, it would get over charged. The reason is to ensure that every buyer is paying same price. This is necessary to have level field in the comp.
.
And the royalties should directly be deposited in the accounts of citizens.
.
Pls see the draft at http://tinyurl.com/DDMRCAM
.
It achieves the same.
.
if you like the draft, then pls send following order to MP via SMS
.
====
Dear MP , If and after majority voters from your area send this link to you via SMS , please implement this order of mine, to print Direct Deposit of Mineral Royalty into Citizens Accounts and Military draft in Gazette give athttp://tinyurl.com/DDMRCAM

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Mar 15 2015 03:48:58 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Can a less unethical law-draft decrease strength of nation wrt another nation and can less unethical law-draft make a nation stronger ? IMO --- less unethical law-draft always increase strength.
.
IMO, strength should be always compared . It is pointless to evaluate strength in isolation. And likewise, whether a law is ethical or unethical should only be compared. i.e. given two law-drafts, which one is less unethcal. Evaluating ethicalness of a law in isolation is pointless.
.
Now say there are two nations --- nation-A and nation-B. Say there are two law-drafts law-X and law-Y wrt same issue. The issue can be anything ---- interest rate or slavery or casteism or inheritance or taxation or military trainning or education.
.
Say Nation-A practices a law-X amongst its citizens.
.
While nation-B practices law-Y .
.
And over time nation-A becomes stronger, may be due to that law-X , and destroys / enslaves nation-B.
.
Now following QUESTIONS come arise
.
(a) if the law-X was more unethical compared to law-Y, then how come that more unethical law made nation-A more powerful than nation-B?
.
(b) if law-X was less unethical , then why didnt nation-B replace its law-Y with law-X ?
.
======
Answered will be subjective because very definition of ethics is
.
There are numerous examples. eg Cetrain nations (or societies) such as islamic societies from 500 AD onwards banned interest on loans citing it as unethical. Certain societies like Hindus of same time said that "capping interest rates or banning interest on loans is unethical , and so unlimited interest rate should be permitted !!"
.
Many , like myself, believe that unrestricted rate of interest was one factor which weakened the Hindus and ban on interest rates strengthened the the then muslims.
.
Likewise, Christians "banned" private ownership of temples and inheritance. While Hindus continued with private ownership of temples and also permitted inheritance. And many , like myself, believed that inheritance and private ownership on temples weakened the Hindu societies.
.
So the question where "are less unethical laws are reasons behind strengths of the societies" and "if so, why other societies didnt accept those laws" become important.
.
I dont see one answer on these questions. But IMO, it is important that each activist decides his answers to these questions --- whatever his answers may be.

Read full post →

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sun Mar 15 2015 05:49:37 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Can a less unethical law-draft decrease strength of nation wrt another nation and can less unethical law-draft make a nation stronger ? IMO --- less unethical law-draft always increase strength.
.
IMO, strength should be always compared . It is pointless to evaluate strength in isolation. And likewise, whether a law is ethical or unethical should only be compared. i.e. given two law-drafts, which one is less unethcal. Evaluating ethicalness of a law in isolation is pointless.
.
Now say there are two nations --- nation-A and nation-B. Say there are two law-drafts law-X and law-Y wrt same issue. The issue can be anything ---- interest rate or slavery or casteism or inheritance or taxation or military trainning or education.
.
Say Nation-A practices a law-X amongst its citizens.
.
While nation-B practices law-Y .
.
And over time nation-A becomes stronger, may be due to that law-X , and destroys / enslaves nation-B.
.
Now following QUESTIONS come arise
.
(a) if the law-X was more unethical compared to law-Y, then how come that more unethical law made nation-A more powerful than nation-B?
.
(b) if law-X was less unethical , then why didnt nation-B replace its law-Y with law-X ?
.
======
Answered will be subjective because very definition of ethics is
.
There are numerous examples. eg Cetrain nations (or societies) such as islamic societies from 500 AD onwards banned interest on loans citing it as unethical. Certain societies like Hindus of same time said that "capping interest rates or banning interest on loans is unethical , and so unlimited interest rate should be permitted !!"
.
Many , like myself, believe that unrestricted rate of interest was one factor which weakened the Hindus and ban on interest rates strengthened the the then muslims.
.
Likewise, Christians "banned" private ownership of temples and inheritance. While Hindus continued with private ownership of temples and also permitted inheritance. And many , like myself, believed that inheritance and private ownership on temples weakened the Hindu societies.
.
So the question where "are less unethical laws are reasons behind strengths of the societies" and "if so, why other societies didnt accept those laws" become important.
.
I dont see one answer on these questions. But IMO, it is important that each activist decides his answers to these questions --- whatever his answers may be.
.

author : RCM rrg

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Mar 15 2015 15:40:24 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

The campaign methods must have features which adds more activists , not just adds citizen-supporters. And adding activists is a hard part because no activist would like to decrease hours he wants to give to his own cause.
.
To elaborate, if there are 1000 activists working for some law-drafts using methods that is increasing number of citizen-supporters , but not adding new activists , then information on law-drafts will NOT reach 45 crore voters , and so the law-drafts may not come.
.
===
.
Dear recallists ,
.
We are all campaigning for one or more law-drafts such as TCP-draft , RTR-drafts over several posts such PM \ CM \ MP \ judges \ PoliceChief etc , MRCM-draft , JurySys-draft, wealth tax draft etc etc.
.
Now all activists may not want to work on all law-drafts. Most activists would like to work ONLY on the law-drafts that reduce the problems close to their heart.
.
Lets label activist-X as activist who is working for law-draft-X. One activist may be working on many law-drafts.
.
The campaign-method-X for law-draft-X means set of steps activist for draft-X are taking to ensure that over 45 crore voters do send orders to MPs via SMS with url to law-draft-X .
.
The steps AFTER 45 crore voters have sent SMS with url-X are AMUS-steps aka Ahimsamurti Mahatma Udham Singh steps , which are NOT part of campaign steps. And I will not talk about them in this post.
.
So campaign method will include steps needed to ensure that 60 crore to 85 crore voters do come to know about the proposed law-draft.
.
Now only two known ways to spread information amongst crores of citizens are --- paidmedia and activists. The activists will use their money to print pamphlets , give newspaper advt etc etc.
.
Since we do not have support for paidmedia, only way we can spread information is via activists.
.
So campaign steps will also include steps taken by activist-X to increase number of activists working for law-draft-X.
.
If number of activists dont increase and remains constant to 1000 or whatever, then they will never be able to reach to 60 crore to 80 crore voters. Because each activist has limited strength.
.
The campaign methods must have features which adds more activists , not just adds citizen-supporters. And adding activists is a hard part because no activist would like to decrease hours he wants to give to his own cause.
.
To elaborate, if there are 1000 activists working for some law-drafts using methods that is increasing number of citizen-supporters , but not adding new activists , then information on law-drafts will NOT reach 45 crore voters , and so the law-drafts may not come.
.
===
.
Dear recallists ,
.
We are all campaigning for one or more law-drafts such as TCP-draft , RTR-drafts over several posts such PM \ CM \ MP \ judges \ PoliceChief etc , MRCM-draft , JurySys-draft, wealth tax draft etc etc.
.
Now all activists may not want to work on all law-drafts. Most activists would like to work ONLY on the law-drafts that reduce the problems close to their heart.
.
Lets label activist-X as activist who is working for law-draft-X. One activist may be working on many law-drafts.
.
The campaign-method-X for law-draft-X means set of steps activist for draft-X are taking to ensure that over 45 crore voters do send orders to MPs via SMS with url to law-draft-X .
.
The steps AFTER 45 crore voters have sent SMS with url-X are AMUS-steps aka Ahimsamurti Mahatma Udham Singh steps , which are NOT part of campaign steps. And I will not talk about them in this post.
.
So campaign method will include steps needed to ensure that 60 crore to 85 crore voters do come to know about the proposed law-draft.
.
Now only two known ways to spread information amongst crores of citizens are --- paidmedia and activists. The activists will use their money to print pamphlets , give newspaper advt etc etc.
.
Since we do not have support for paidmedia, only way we can spread information is via activists.
.
So campaign steps will also include steps taken by activist-X to increase number of activists working for law-draft-X.
.
If number of activists dont increase and remains constant to 1000 or whatever, then they will never be able to reach to 60 crore to 80 crore voters. Because each activist has limited strength.
.
SO WHATEVER CAMPAIGN METHODS YOU HAVE TAKEN, PLEASE MAKE SURE THAT YOUR METHODS ARE ADDING ACTIVISTS , NOT JUST SUPPORTERS
.
Now here is the main problem that most activists are already committed to one ore more cause. An activist may work on law-drafts that address his cause, but not on law-drafts that address other causes !! And "converting" activist i.e. asking him to give even one hour a week to other causes is next to impossible.
.
This is the central problem in true-activism ---- that an activist working for law-draft-X would NOT give even one hour to a law-draft for cause Y. In such case , how can 20 lakh activists be convinced to work on any law-draft-X?
.
So summarizing
.
1. To inform 60 crore voters about a law-draft-X , some 2 lakh to 20 lakh activists would be needed to work on law-draft-X
.
2. The campaign methods activists-X must have features that will not only get followers but also get more activists.
.
3. It isnt easy to get activists for a cause, because almost all activists are already working on some cause, and an activist working on one cause will not be willing to work on other cause.
.
I will later write my views on how (3) can be overcome.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Mar 16 2015 11:00:35 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

A useful historical fact --- by 1870 , London School Board members and Chairman were all elected , and elected by citizens across London. Back then, MPs were elected by only 10% citizens i.e. only 10% had voting rights in election of MPs. But for school board members, voting was with almost 100% citizens.
.
Also " Firstly, the board's election of 1870 was polled by secret ballot, being the first large-scale election to use this approach in Britain. Secondly, the cumulative voting system gave electors a number of votes equal to the number of seats in the division in which they were voting. The elector could use up as many of their votes on a single candidate as they wished, which meant that minority interests often found representation."
.
see wiki for http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/London_School_Board for more
.
This vastly improved education.
.
USA went one step further and also enacted Right to recall over District Education Officer. And so education in USA became better than education than UK.
.
In India, citizens have near zero control over District Education Officers and other authorities in education.
.
I at Right to recall group along with some 1000 recallists across India have been campaigning for Right to Recall over DEO = District Education Officer.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Mar 16 2015 11:11:34 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Social mobility is best measured by NOT how many kids of poor parents became rich --- but how many kids from poor parents , who were govt employees , became engineers , scientists etc .
.
Parental background of all engineers , doctors , scientists below age of 30 years in India, China , Russia, USA, China India as of today should be tabulated.
.
The gut feeling I am having is that kids of poor , except govt employees, have stopped becoming engineers, scientists, doctors etc etc. Many of them do become rich --- but that is via real estate deals, businesses, govt contracts, politics etc etc and not via education, Becoming rich via any fair means is welcome, and no profession is wrong. But "why kids from poor families are no longer becoming engineering , scientists , doctors etc" is an important question.
.
The answer is simple --- the education in municipal schools has been going from bad to worse to worst.
.
Solution is also simple --- Right to recall district education officer. Across world, education for poor improved ONLY after education offices became "political" !! eg Education in London and UK took boost ONLY in after 1870 when schools board members / chairman were elected by ALL citizens across London !! Back then, MPs were elected by mere 10% citizens , but the Chairman of London School were elected by all citizens (see wiki at http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/London_School_Board ) . And USA took it to next level by creating RTR over their District Education Officer.
.
In India, most education volunteers oppose RTR over DEO !!! (guess why?) And SoMoKe have opposed RTR-DEO for past 40 years (guess why) . And their andbhagats have also opposed RTR-DEO (guess why).
. RSS-apex too has always opposed RTR-DEO (pls ask them). Their pet argument is --- RTR-DEO will bring instability in India !!!
.
If IYO RTR-DEO is good procedure, then you are doing immense damage by supporting CoBhAp-apex or RSS-apex. If you want RTR-DEO in India, then you must inform ALL the FACT that SoMoKe are opposing RTR-DEO procedure. This will weaken SoMoKe and also force them to bring RTR-DEO procedure in India. Ghee sidhi ungali se nahi nikalata.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Mar 16 2015 12:54:24 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

RSS confirms Ram temple is on the back burner . And no statemenst on Krisna Janam Bhoomi and Kashi Vishvanath
.
http://timesofindia.indiatimes.com/india/RSS-confirms-Ram-temple-is-on-the-back-burner/articleshow/46577067.cms
.
NAGPUR: Newly re-elected sarkaryavah (general secretary) of the Rashtriya Swayamsevak Sangh (RSS) Bhaiyyaji Joshi has said the Sangh would not resort to agitation mode for now to achieve its ultimate goal of building a Ram temple at Ayodhya. "The matter is before the Supreme Court. We would rather wait for a judicial resolution rather than taking any hasty public action," said Joshi on Sunday, a day after he was elected for the third straight term of three years as the number 2 in the RSS.

For years, the RSS has harped on the temple issue, calling it a matter of faith on which even courts had no jurisdiction. Its political offspring, the BJP, came into electoral reckoning after the Babri Masjid was demolished on the promise that an imposing temple would take its place. Over the years, the temple theme has fallen off BJP's election manifestos. Now, the Hindutva fountainhead also seems to be conveniently forgetting the 'faith factor' and is willing to patiently wait for the apex court to resolve the legal tangle. As Joshi said, the RSS in no hurry or mood for a public agitation over the temple.

(I got a request from assistant of a senior Gujarat leader that he wanted some important points wrt science \ tech in Gujarat ; My response )
.
1. The most important is that Maths \ Science Education is detoriating in many schools and particularly Municipal Schools. Gujarat Govt must make a system to record where the municipal school students and students from ordinary private schools are lending after 5-10 years after they pass out. We should gather data on --- of the students who passed from Municipal Schools and Govt Schools in years say from 1995 to say 2008 , how of them
.
(a) took enrollment in class-`12 science
(b) got enrollment in Bsc, Engineering , MBBS etc
.
Same way, Govt should get this information for all other schools.
.
======
.
2. Gujarat Board should gather information on how many students of Gujarat board are enrolling in IIT , IISc , Engineering , MBBS and compare it with other boards such as CBSE and ICSE.
.
======
.
3. Most important way to improve sciences is ti make factories more and more competitive . And today, biggest problems factories are facing is -- sluggishness in courts. USA has one judge for population of 11,000 , while Gujarat has one judge for population of about 65000 . Gujarat has about 900 lower courts , while it needs at least about 5000 . Increase in number of courts will speed up the cases , will improve efficiency of factory owners will increase. This will increase competition and increase the demand of engineers as well as scientists
.
======
.
4. Gujarat Govt should organize competitions in Maths and Science similar to "Maths Olympiads" .
.
=====
.
5. Gujarat Govt should print bilingual textbooks i.e. textbooks should be in both languages --- Gujarati and English. The Left page can be in English and the Right page should be its exact translation. This will improve English of students at early age.
.
These are some of the tasks , that be easily done to promote science and technology in Gujarat .
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Wed Mar 18 2015 05:51:04 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

क्या हमें सभी बहुराष्ट्रीय कंपनियों का विरोध करना चाहिए ?
नहीं ।
तो हमें कौनसी बहुराष्ट्रीय कंपनियों का विरोध करना चाहिए, और क्यों ?
.

माइक्रोसोफ्ट, मोटोरोला, इंटेल तथा इन जैसी सैकड़ो कम्पनियां औचित्य पूर्ण ढंग से संपत्तियों की रचना करती है, किन्तु कई भ्रष्ट बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियाँ जिनमे कुछ भारतीय शीर्ष कम्पनियां भी शामिल है, बेंक, खनिज, इलेक्ट्रोनिक प्रिंट मिडिया आदि को कब्ज़े में लेने के एजेंडे पर कार्य करती है, जो कि भविष्य में देश की आर्थिक एवं सुरक्षा व्यवस्था को गंभीर क्षति पहुचाती है । ऐसी कम्पनियां देश में सम्पत्तियो की रचना नहीं बल्कि उनका हरण करती है ।

यहाँ तक कि अच्छी बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियों को भी संवेदनशील क्षेत्रो में व्यापार की अनुमति देना हमारी सुरक्षा व्यवस्था को गहरे जोखिम में डाल सकता है ।
उदाहरण के लिए, मान लीजिये कि हम एक अमेरिकी कंपनी नोर्टेल से संचार उपकरण आयात करते है तथा इन आयातित उपकरणों में ये विदेशी कम्पनियाँ जिन रेड़ियो रिसीवर का प्रयोग करती है, उन्हें सिग्नल भेजकर निष्क्रिय किया जा सकता है, तो अमेरिका से युद्ध के दौरान अमुक कम्पनी सिर्फ एक बटन दबा कर हमारे पूरे संचार नेटवर्क को ठप कर सकती है, जिससे हमारी सेना आपस के सम्प्रेषण से वंचित हो जायेगी और हमें युद्ध में हार का सामना करना पड़ेगा । इसीलिए उच्च तकनीक से सम्बंधित ऐसे उपकरण जिनसे कि हमारी सुरक्षा में सेंध लगाई जा सकती है, हमें किसी भी प्रकार की विदेशी कम्पनी से आयात करने से बचना चाहिए ।

अत: हमें ऐसी राजपत्र अधिसूचना की आवश्यकता है, जो कि व्यापार के लिए उन अच्छी कंपनियों को व्यापार की अनुमति दे जो कि रोजगार, सम्पति तथा समृद्धि की रचना करती है, न कि उन बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियों को जो कि एकाधिकार स्थापित करके राष्ट्रीय संपदाओ को हड़प लेती है । साथ ही हमें ऐसी अधिसूचना की भी आवश्यकता है, जो यह सुनिश्चित करे कि अच्छी कंपनियों से होने वाले नुक्सान को भी कम किया जा सके ।

हमें बहुराष्ट्रीय कंपनियों के नियंत्रण को कम करने के लिए क्या उपाय करने चाहिए ?

इसके लिए जरुरी है कि हम अपनी उत्पादकता और तकनिकी कुशलता को बढाए तथा यह भी सुनिश्चित करे कि बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियों के देश में बढ़ते प्रभाव को कम किया जा सके ।
हमारी उत्पादकता और ताकत बढाने के लिए हमें राईट टू रिकाल, ज्यूरी सिस्टम, वेल्थ टेक्स, आदि कानूनों की आव्श्यकता है, जिससे हमारी न्याय व्यवस्था, पुलिस प्रशासन तथा राजनेतिक क्षेत्र की कार्यकुशलता में वृद्धि हो । साथ ही हमें ऐसे कानूनों की भी आवश्यकता है जो कि भ्रष्ट बहुराष्ट्रीय कंपनियों के भारत में बढ़ते प्रभाव पर लगाम लगा सके ।

ध्यान देने योग्य तथ्य यह है कि, यदि हम बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियों को खदेड़ देते है, किन्तु ऐसे कानूनों को लागू नही करते है जिनसे हमारी सैन्य शक्ति बढ़े, तो अमेरिकी सेना के आक्रमण का हम सामना नही कर पायेंगे और हार जायेंगे । क्योंकि यह तय बात है कि, यदि हम बहुराष्ट्रीय कंपनियों को देश में व्यापार के नाम पर लूटने की खुली छूट नही देते है, तो हमें उनकी सैन्य हमला झेलना पड़ेगा ।
उदाहरण के लिए मान लीजिये कि हम भ्रष्ट कम्पनियों को क़ानून बना कर देश से बाहर निकाल देते है, किन्तु जब इन कंपनियों के प्रोत्साहन से अमेरिकी सेना हमला करेगी तो हम इन्हें किसी भी क़ानून द्वारा नही रोक सकेंगे। ऐसी परिस्थिति में देश को सिर्फ स्वदेशी हथियार और सैन्य शक्ति ही बचा सकती है । यदि हमें स्वदेशी आधुनिक हथियारों का उत्पादन करना है तो हमें न्याय व्यवस्था और भ्रष्टाचार को दुरुस्त करने के लिए राईट टू रिकाल और ज्यूरी सिस्टम जैसे कानूनों की आवश्यकता है, अन्यथा इन कम्पनियों को रोकना हमें क्षणिक लाभ ही देगा ।

.

क्षमा कीजिये, आपके फोन में आउट गोइंग कॉल की सुविधा नहीं है !!!

यदि नेता कुछ कहना चाहता है तो रेली कर देता है, साक्षात्कार दे देता है, प्रेस कोंफ्रेंस बुला लेता है, टीवी बहस में हिस्सा ले लेता है, संसद में भासण कर देता ।
जनता यदि सुनना चाहे तो सुनना है, और न सुनना चाहे तो भी सुनना है । जब नही सुनना है, तो भी नेता ने सुनाना है तो सुना ही देता है ।
.

लेकिन यदि जनता ( यहाँ जनता से आशय करोड़ो नागरिक है, न कि एक या मुठ्ठी भर व्यक्ति ) अपने नेता से कुछ कहना चाहे तो जनता ( we the people ) के पास क्या विकल्प है ?

अनशन करो, धरने दो, प्रदर्शन करो, ( नेता उवाच - जनता हमारे साथ है )

पटरियां उखाड़ो, पुतले जलाओ, रास्ते जाम करो, ( नेता उवाच - यह विपक्ष की साज़िश है )

हस्ताक्षर अभियान चलाओ, सोशल मिडिया पर चीं चां करो, ज्ञापन दो ( अनाधिकृत और असंगठित तरीके )
.

भारत में करोड़ो नागरिको के पास अपनी मांग स्पष्ट, अधिकृत, पारदर्शी, तथा सम्मिलित रूप से रखने की कोई शांतिपूर्ण और कानूनी प्रक्रिया नहीं है । ये एक तरफ़ा संवाद सत्ता को लाभ देता है जबकि नागरिको को नुकसान ।
.

समाधान : हमें एक ऐसे क़ानून की आवश्यकता है, जिसके तहत नागरिको को यह अधिकार मिले कि :

यदि कोई भी नागरिक प्रधानमन्त्री या मुख्यमंत्री की वेबसाईट पर कोई सुझाव, शिकायत या प्रस्ताव दर्ज करना चाहता है,तो उसे ऐसा करने दिया जाए ।

तथा यदि कोई भी नागरिक ऐसी किसी शिकायत, सुझाव या प्रस्ताव पर अपनी हाँ या ना दर्ज करना चाहता है तो उसे ऐसा करने दिया जाए ।

यदि करोड़ो नागरिक किसी शिकायत पर हाँ दर्ज कर देते है, तो प्रधानमन्त्री उस पर कार्यवाही कर सकते है ।
.

राईट टू रिकाल ग्रुप ने ऐसी प्रक्रिया की रचना के लिए कानूनी ड्राफ्ट प्रस्तावित किया है, जिसे गेजेट में प्रकाशित करके इस क़ानून को भारत में लागू किया जा सकता है । यदि आप चाहते है कि आपकी आवाज अधिकृत, स्पष्ट और पारदर्शी ढंग से सरकार के समक्ष रख सके, और आपको अनशन धरने जाम प्रदर्शन ज्ञापन आदि से छुटकारा मिले, तो अपने क्षेत्र के सांसद को SMS द्वारा इस क़ानून को गेजेट में छापने का आदेश देवें । हम इस क़ानून को टी सी पी ( ट्रांसपरेंस कम्प्लेन प्रोसीजर ) के नाम से पुकारते है ।
.

टी सी पी क़ानून के ड्राफ्ट का लिंक और SMS का नमूना कमेन्ट बॉक्स में देखें ।
.

हम विधायक चुनते है, विधायक सीएम को चुनते है । सीएम की कृपा पर गृहमंत्री नियुक्त होता है, गृहमंत्री की मर्जी पर एसपी की नौकरी चलती है ।
जनता न विधायक को बदल सकती है, न मुख्यमंत्री को, न गृहमंत्री को, न ही एसपी को । पांच साल तक ये चारो मिलकर लोकतंत्र लोकतंत्र खेलते है, और जनता तमाशा देखती है ।

विधायक + मुख्यमंत्री + गृहमंत्री + एसपी = लोकतंत्र ( लोक कहाँ है ? )
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Mar 21 2015 09:50:15 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

referendum is legal in india
.
Pls see
.
http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Goa_Opinion_Poll
.
The Goa Opinion Poll was a referendum held in the state of Goa, India, on 16 January 1967, to decide the future of the Union Territory of Goa, Daman and Diu within the Indian Union. Although popularly called an opinion poll, it was in fact, a referendum, as the results of the poll were binding on the government of India. The referendum offered the people of Goa a choice between continuing as a union territory or merging with the state of Maharashtra. It is the only referendum to have been held in independent India.[1][2][3] The people of Goa voted against the merger and Goa continued to be a union territory. Subsequently, in 1987, Goa became a full-fledged state within the Indian Union.
.
===========================
.
So BJP can also take nationwide referendum on art-370 without Parliament's permission.

IMO , not marrying or marrying has NOTHING to do with level of commitment and makes ZERO difference on well being of nation / community ---- a
comparision across countries.
.
Many times , many people take "not marrying" as a sign of great sacrifice and great commitment. And by same token, when asked "does marriage mean lesser commitment?" , they cant give clear answer as that answer will offend majority.
.
Across time , across countries --- there is NO correlation that unmarried men did lesser or more good that married ones. Many sects such Catholics etc take pride in the fact that their priests dont marry and thus cite it as proof of their commitment !! But Protestant priests marry and generally they are supposed to get married as well as have children !! And Protestants too do well --- as well as Catholics. In fact, protestants in general are economically better off and more educated than Catholics !! The difference has NOTHING to do with the religion IMO --- it is due to other reasons.
.
In India, , some sects allow priests to marry while most dont. And the Indian "panth" , which has been strongest and done best to protect India since its inception --- Sikhism --- allows marriages of all their religious figures from Guru to Gurudwara Prabandhak to Bedi to Granthi to Raagi etc (Bedi was once a title in Sikh --- meaning someone who knows Vedas i.e. somone who is very knowledgeable in all matters). . The Nihangs warriors often remain unmarried but there is NO strict rule that they must remain unmarried. They can marry, have children and they can also also opt out of Nihang ranks , which is very very rare. (please google on Nihang marry and "can nihang marry"). Nihangs often dont marry --- but that is pure choice.
.

.

But marriage was discourage and only unmarried got promotions. And finally, in 300 AD , marriage was abolished for all priests by the then Catholic high council. The reason given was --- Jesus was unmarried , which itself is unverified and now unverifiable truth !! The novels like "Da Vince Code" say that Jesus was "married" and also had children !!! The Pracharak is required to be unmarried , except in very rare cases. And only unmarried Pracharaks qualify for promotion and responsible assignments.
.
In India, there is belief in many voters that unmarried / childless person would be LESS dishonest that a married one !!! This is opposite of West. !!! In fact , unmarried / childless wont qualify for even corporate high posts --- exceptions exist. In West, unmarried / childless is NOT trustworthy !!!

And movie Interstellar also pushes this Western "protestant" belief that unmarried men can be more selfish and married with children will be LESS selfish !!! (pls google in Dr Mann Interstellar and see Interstellar again !!)

In fact, if 1 crore men are told that "you will get promotion and recognition only if you remain unmarried", then well, some 1 lakh may decide to remain unmarried but that may be because they wanted promotion and recognition , not out of their wish. So in any case, not marrying isnt any "proof" of commitment as many tend to think.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sat Mar 21 2015 17:08:36 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

चिंटू : मास्टर जी, सरकार अपने खर्चे के लिए पैसा कहाँ से लाती है ?

मास्टर जी : सरकार टेक्स से पैसा जुटाती है ।
.

चिंटू : क्या खनन से प्राप्त रोयल्टी भी टेक्स है ?

मास्टर जी : नहीं यह राष्ट्र की संपत्ति है, टेक्स नहीं । सरकारों को अपने खर्चे टेक्स वसूल करके ही जुटाने चाहिए, संपत्ति बेच कर नहीं ।
.

चिंटू : लेकिन राष्ट्र की संपत्ति भी तो सरकार की संपत्ति ही है ।

मास्टर जी : ऐसा नही है । सरकार एक व्यवस्था है जिसे राष्ट्र चलाने की ज़िम्मेदारी दी जाती है । राष्ट्र करोड़ो नागरिको की मिलकियत है, अत: इस रोयल्टी पर नागरिको का हक है ।
.

चिंटू : मास्टर जी, खनन क्षेत्र में इतना भ्रष्टाचार क्यों सुनने में आता है ?

मास्टर जी : क्योंकि इस विपुल सम्पदा का कोई मालिक नही । नागरिको को यह जानकारी ही नही है, कि खनिज और प्राकृतिक संसाधन उनकी संपत्ति है, अत: नेता, नौकरशाह और धनिक इफरात से बिखरी पड़ी इस संपदा को औने पौने दामो में निकाल कर आपस में बाँट लेते है, और अवाम इस फ़िक्र में मुब्तिला रहती है कि प्रधानमन्त्री जी का बंद गले का सूट कितनी राशि में नीलाम हुआ ।
.

चिंटू : इसे रोकने का उपाय क्या है ?

मास्टर जी : हमें ऐसे क़ानून की जरुरत है, जिसके अनुसार खनिजो और प्राकृतिक संसाधनों से प्राप्त रोयल्टी सीधे नागरिको के खाते में जमा की जाए ।
.

चिंटू : ऐसा होने से क्या लाभ होंगे ।

मास्टर जी : जब रोयल्टी सीधे नागरिको के खाते में जमा होने लगेगी तो हर नागरिक को लगभग 400-500 रू प्रतिमाह मिलने लगेंगे, जिससे गरीबी दूर होगी, नागरिक नीलामी की प्रक्रिया में रुचि लेंगे जिससे पारदर्शिता आएगी और भ्रष्टाचार कम होगा।
.

चिंटू : क्या इस व्यवस्था को लागू करने का कोई कानूनी ड्राफ्ट उपलब्ध है ?

मास्टर जी : हाँ, राईट टू रिकाल ग्रुप ने 'डीडी एमआर सीएम' नाम का क़ानून प्रस्तावित किया है, जो कि दिए गए लिंक पर उपलब्ध है :
tinyurl .com/NagrikAamdani
.

चिंटू : कैसे भारत में यह क़ानून लागू हो सकता है ?

मास्टर जी : चिंटू, यदि करोड़ो नागरिक अपने क्षेत्र के सांसद को SMS भेज कर टी सी पी के ड्राफ्ट को गेजेट में प्रकाशित करने की मांग करे, तो प्रधानमन्त्री टी सी पी क़ानून को गेजेट में प्रकाशित कर देंगे । टी सी पी क़ानून आने पर DDMRCAM के क़ानून को प्रधानमन्त्री की वेबसाईट पर दर्ज किया जा सकेगा, ताकि करोड़ो नागरिक इस क़ानून पर अपनी सहमती दर्ज कर सके ।
.

चिंटू : क्या बिना टी सी पी के इस क़ानून को पास करने का कोई तरीका नही है ?

मास्टर जी : बिलकुल है । यदि करोडो नागरिक अपने सांसद को sms द्वारा आदेश देते है कि DDMRCAM क़ानून गेजेट में छापा जाए, तो प्रधानमन्त्री इस क़ानून को गेजेट में प्रकाशित कर देंगे ।
.

चिंटू : लेकिन सरकार से तो कोई भी ऐसी मांग करता नज़र नहीं आ रहा । सब कोई तो क्रिकेट, फिल्म स्टार, आयुर्वेद, योग, साफ़ सफाई, झाडू, टोपी, नारे, भाषण, प्रवचन, आदि में व्यस्त है ।

मास्टर जी : चिंटू, सभी व्यक्ति अपनी रुचि और लाभालाभ के अनुसार व्यवहार करते है, अत: जिसे रोयल्टी में अपना हिस्सा चाहिए वो इसमें रुचि लेगा जिसे नही है, वो नही लेगा, वरना नेता तो इसमें रुचि ले ही रहे है ।

NaMo orders 74% to 100% FDI in railway engine manufacturing in India --- SoGa / ArKe support it , and so do CoBhApSwaRa-workers !!!
.
http://indiatoday.intoday.in/story/suresh-prabhu-fdi-plans-make-in-india-manufacturing-plants-bihar-locomotive-diesel/1/422794.html
.
Suresh Prabhu flags off FDI plans, Rs 2400 crore to be spent on manufacturing plants in Bihar .
.
Railway minister Suresh Prabhu has finally given the go-ahead to the longstalled, big-ticket foreign direct investment (FDI) projects to set up state-of-the art diesel and electric locomotive manufacturing plants in Bihar in collaboration with multinational giants. The move forms part of the strategy to boost the 'Make in India' campaign.The plan for setting up the two locomotive plants was first announced by then-railway minister Lalu Prasad Yadav in 2008 but got stuck due to delays and repeated revision of bidding documents. A senior railway official confirmed that the Railways has finalised the financial bidding for the projects after a lot of rethinking and prolonged due diligence. .....
.... The Railways will have 26-per cent equity while global players will have 74-per cent equity in each of the plants at Madhepura and Marhora. The government had approved 100-per cent FDI in the Railways and setting up of the two locomotive plants as a joint venture will help to modernise the public sector behemoth and boost the country's infrastructure. ......
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sat Mar 21 2015 18:21:11 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

मोदीजी के आदेश से होगा 74% से 100% विदेशी निवेश कृत रेलवे उत्पादन। सोनिया गांधी और केजरीवाल ने सहमति जताई। अतः कांग्रेस/भाजपा/आप - कार्यकर्ताओं ने भी सहमति में सर हिलाया।
.
http://indiatoday.intoday.in/story/suresh-prabhu-fdi-plans-make-in-india-manufacturing-plants-bihar-locomotive-diesel/1/422794.html
.
सुरेश प्रभु ने FDI योजना को हरी झण्डी दी। 2400/- करोड़ रुपये का प्रत्यक्ष विदेशी निवेश हुआ बिहार में रेलवे उत्पादन फैक्ट्री के लिए बिहार में।
.
रेलवे मंत्री सुरेश प्रभु ने अंततः अतिप्रतीक्षित FDI योजना के तहत डीजल और विद्युत इंजन उत्पादन फैक्ट्री को बिहार में विदेशी व्यापारियों के गठजोड़ से क्रियान्वयन शुरू करने के आदेश दिए। यह कदम "मेक इन इंडिया" नीति के तहत लिया गया है। पूर्व में प्रकार के 2 प्लांट शुरू करने की घोषणा 2008 में तत्कालीन रेलवे मंत्री लालू प्रसाद यादव ने करी थी जो की अति शंशोधन और बोली प्रक्रिया में फसे रहने की वजह से लंबित थी। अब लालू जी का अटका काम पूरा होगा। एक प्रमुख रेलवे अधिकारी ने स्पष्ट किया की काफी जद्दोजहद के बाद अंततः रेलवे विभाग ने इस विदेशी परियोजना के लिए वित्त बोली प्रक्रिया पूरी कर ली है।
.
इस परियोजना में रेलवे के पास केवल 26% शेयर होगा और दूसरी तरफ 74% विदेशी व्यापारियों का कब्ज़ा रहेगा, मधेपुरा और मरहोरा प्लांट्स में। सरकार ने रेलवे में 100% विदेशी निवेश करने की ठान ली है, ताकि तकनिकी विकास हो और देश का बुनियादी ढांचा विकसित हो सके।

  • http://indiatoday.intoday.in/story/suresh-prabhu-fdi-plans-make-in-india-manufacturing-plants-bihar-locomotive-diesel/1/422794.html
  • https://external-phl2-1.xx.fbcdn.net/emg1/v/t13/5858444414587614191?url=http%3A%2F%2Fmedia2.intoday.in%2Findiatoday%2Fimages%2Fstories%2Fprabhu-3_350_030915092141.jpg&fb_obo=1&utld=intoday.in&_nc_sid=632586&_nc_ht=external-phl2-1.xx.fbcdn.net&stp=c63.0.225.225a_dst-jpg_flffffff_q75_s139x139_tt6&ccb=18-1&_nc_gid=YsXU9BkrAsr1DOuS8fKB5g&_nc_zt=3&oh=06_Q3_DAfAD0O4OehHCkhDvRC4S-TpFQzhLalwMRYJFc77bNqIT&oe=6AC31C1D

1) Should cities in India have no left turn on red traffic light?
.
My answer is --- LEFT TURN ON RED SHOULD END. i.e. NO LEFT TURN ON RED
.
Please post answers on YOUR wall, not here .
.
To know what is "turning on red light" , pls google.
.
But if you are "responsible" citizen ,its time you at least make up your mind on whether left turn on red should be allowed or not.
.
=====
.
2) Should footpaths have trees?
.
My answer is ---- Foothpaths below 6 feet of width MUST NOT have trees.
,
================
.
3. Should gates of an apartment complex be allowed to open outwards and block footpaths
.
My answer --- NO. The gates MUST open inside the perimeter
.
================
.
4) Should hawkers be allowed on foothpaths
.
My answer --- The Jurors should decide. And if I am in Jury, my answer will be NO
.
================
.
5) Should footpaths have boxes for electricity and phones?
.
My answer --- Foothpaths below 6 feet of width MUST NOT have any such boxes. If there is no space, then space from near by plot should be bought at market rate.
.
============================
.
6) should vehicles be allowed to cover zebra crossing during red light?
.
My answer --- (after zebra crossings are made !!). NO. The Jury should decide penalty. And if I am in Jury , penalty will be 2 hours of detention, with 2 extra hours each offence. (I oppose financial fines and support detention only).
.
======
.
All in all if you claim that you have traffic sense, please post answer to above questions relating pedestarians on YOUR wall,. You may have any answers you like (eg pedestarians should not exist or pedestarians should walk in sky and not disturb vehicles etc etc). But I request you all to alteast spell out all questions and answers.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sun Mar 22 2015 07:14:04 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

किराए की कोख :

मोदी साहेब ने रेलवे में 75 से 100% FDI की अनुमति दे दी है
.

मधेपुरा और महरोरा में रेल कारखानों में अब विदेशी कम्पनियां इंजन और डब्बे बनाएगी । भारतीय रेल का इसमें हिस्सा 0% से 25% रहेगा ।
विदेशी कम्पनियां जो निवेश करेगी उनके कमाए गए रूपये का डॉलर हमें देना होगा । हम पहले से ही डॉलर के कर्जे में डूबे हुए है, अब स्थिति और बदतर हो जायेगी ।
.

हम अभी राजधानी, शताब्दी और दुरंतो के इंजन इम्पोर्ट कर रहे है, जिन्हें कि भारत में भी बनाया जा सकता है । किन्तु डिब्बे और पटरियां हम खुद ही बना रहे है, अत: हमें डॉलर सिर्फ इंजन के लिए ही खर्च करने होते है । अब डिब्बे पटरियां और अन्य उपकरण भी विदेशी कम्पनियां बनाएगी तो हमें तिहरा नुक्सान होगा । क्योंकि कंपनी जो मुनाफा कमाएगी उनका हमें डॉलर देना होगा । लेकिन हमारे पास डॉलर छापने की मशीने नही है, हम पहले से ही काफी कर्जे लेकर खा चुके है, और हमारा निर्यात भी गिर रहा है । परिणामस्वरूप हमें कर्जे चुकाने के लिए अपने खनिज और प्राकृतिक संसाधन विदेशियों के हाथो बेचने होंगे ।
.

भारतीय रेल आइन्दा के लिए इंजन तथा अन्य तकनिकी उपकरणों के विकास की क्षमता खो देगी, जिससे हम रेल जैसे विषय में भी आत्मनिर्भरता खो देंगे । क्योंकि विदेशी कम्पनियाँ मोनोपोली स्थापित करने के लिए हमारे नेताओं, नौकरशाहो और न्यायलयो को घूस देकर प्रतिस्पर्धी संस्थानों को तबाह कर देगी । ऐसा करने के लिए उन्हें भ्रष्ट नेताओं की जरुरत है, और हमारे नेता आदि कितने ईमानदार है यह विचार करने का भी विषय नही है ।
.

भारतीय रेल अब भारतीय रेल नही रह जायेगी, हम मेक इन इण्डिया के नाम पर अपनी रेल बेच रहे है, जो कि शर्मनाक है ।
.

करीब डेढ़ सौ साल पहले ब्रिटिश यहाँ रेल लेकर आये थे, 1947 में हमने कहा कि अपना देश हम खुद चलाएंगे । हमने राष्ट्रीय गर्व के प्रतीक 'मेड इन इंडिया' का नारा बुलन्द किया, किन्तु हमारे 56 इंची सीना धारको ने 'मेक इन इंडिया' के नाम पर फिर से विदेशियों को रेल का कारोबार संभालने के लिए बुला लिया है ।
यह बात समझ से बाहर है कि हमें किस पर गर्व करना चाहिए और किस पर शर्मिंदा होना चाहिए ।
.

'भारत में क्षमता है' का नारा लगाने वाले सभी राजनेतिक दलों और शीर्ष नेताओं ने हमेशा उन कानूनों का विरोध किया है, जिससे भारत अपने बूते तकनिकी विकास कर के आत्मनिर्भरता हासिल कर सके । यदि हम वेल्थ टेक्स, ज्यूरी सिस्टम और राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों को लागू करते है, तो भारत दुनिया की हर वो चीज बना सकता है जो कि दुनिया के अन्य देश बनाते है । किन्तु मोदी-मनमोहन से लेकर सोनिया-केजरीवाल तक सभी नेता इन कानूनों का विरोध कर रहे है ताकि ताकत सत्ता प्रतिष्ठानों के हाथ में रहे ।
.

ज्यूरी सिस्टम और राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों के अभाव में हम लगातार तकनिकी क्षमता खो रहे है और हमारी सेना कमजोर हो रही है । रेल, हाईवे, टीवी, कम्पूटर, मोबाइल, पावर प्लांट यहाँ तक कि दोयम दर्जे के हथियारों के लिए भी हम विदेशियों पर निर्भर है, और यह निर्भरता लगातार बढ़ रही है । हम रेल और कारो के इंजन विदेशियों से बनवा रहे है, इसलिए फाइटर प्लेन का इंजन बनाने के बारे में सोचते भी नही है । बनाना तो दूर की कौड़ी है ।

.

कार्यकर्ताओं और नागरिको को चाहिए कि शासन से वेल्थ टेक्स, ज्यूरी सिस्टम तथा राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों की मांग करे जिससे भारत खुद तकनीक का विकास कर आत्मनिर्भर बन सके ।

__________________

But they are under control of USA / China . They are just mercenaries for hire and nothing more. The threat of Saudi Arabia / Pakistan is ONLY because China / USA are prompting them to hit India .
.
2) Today , the real power to USUK comes due to their Jury System , Right to Recall , elections for over 30 posts at District / State / National levels etc. This basic changes have created wealth tax, no excise, no sales tax, no vat etch which further strenthened USA. And IMO, we cant survive against march of USA unless we too copy these procedures into India.
.
3) China's progress is because across administration they recruit via broad based written exam which test for raw merit and NOT language , NOT specialized knowledge and NOT interviews !!! But China has no broad based real elections no RTR, weak Jury System and so China is weaker compared to USA and will lose , unless they adopt all these points. We can survive against China unless we at least adopt recruitment by written exams
.
===========
.
So my summary of History is ---- strength of Militaries dont come from strategic thinking or culture or tall leaders but come from administrative systems. The activists can run the systems. The most activists can do is to force tall leaders to change administrative systems and then administrative systems will do the rest. Can tall leaders change administrative systems in absence of sheer force from activists? My answer is ---- NO. Tall leaders only look for short term path of least resistance and have tendency to side with strongest players unless, unless activists force them not to !!
.
So AFAIS = as far as I see, we dont have much choices. If we dont bring Jury System , RTR , recruitment with NO interviews and by written exams , broad based elections, wealth tax , abolish vat / gst etc , then the strength ratio of USA/ India and China / India will keep worsening day by day.
.
So activists should focus on administrative systems which can reduce weaknesses and force tall leaders to print drafts that bring those administrative systems.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sun Mar 22 2015 10:13:45 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

सरकार सभी दलों की सहमती से ऐसा क़ानून लाने की तैयारी में है जिसके लागू होने से सभी दल मिलजुल कर सरकार बना सकेंगे या फिर राष्ट्रपति शासन लागू होगा ।
.

राईट टू रिकाल ग्रुप यह मांग कर रहा है कि यदि नेता भ्रष्टाचार करता है, तो जनता के पास किसी भी समय उसे नौकरी से निकालने का अधिकार हो, जबकि राजनेतिक दल यह तय करने में लगे हुए है कि पांच साल से पहले उन्हें जनता की सूरत किसी भी हाल में न देखनी पड़े ।
.

संसदीय सीमिति ने पूरे देश में लोकसभा और विधानसभा चुनाव एक साथ करवाने का प्रस्ताव किया है, जिस पर 18 राज्यों की सरकारे राजी है ।
.
[ खबर का लिंक कमेन्ट बॉक्स में देखें ]
.

तात्कालीन व्यवस्था यह है कि, सभी राज्यों और केंद्र में स्वतंत्र रूप से चुनावी प्रक्रिया अपनाई जाती है । यदि राज्य या केंद्र सरकारों में किसी पार्टी को बहुमत न मिले तो, पार्टियाँ गठबंधन कर के सरकारों का गठन कर लेती है । किन्तु जब गठबंधन टूट जाता है, तो या तो मध्यावधि चुनाव होते है, या फिर राष्ट्रपति शासन लगाया जाता है । पश्चात सुविधा के अनुसार चुनाव आयोग अमुक राज्य या केंद्र में चुनाव संपन्न कराता है ।
.

उल्लेखनीय है कि राष्ट्रपति शासन को लोकतंत्र का निलम्बन माना जाता है, अत: सम्बंधित राजनेतिक दल और कार्यकर्ताओं द्वारा जल्दी चुनाव कराने की मांग की जाती है ।
.

अब राजनेतिक दल यह सुनिश्चित करना चाहते है कि उन्हें 5 वर्ष से पहले जनता के बीच वोट मांगने नही जाना पड़े । इसके लिए सभी राजनेतिक दल सहमती बनाने की तैयारी में है ।
.

नयी व्यवस्था के तहत निम्न फार्मूले सुझाए जा रहे है :

1. 2019 में लोकसभा चुनाव के साथ-साथ 12 राज्यों में चुनाव होने हैं। जबकि यूपी का चुनाव 2017 में है। इसे एक साथ कराने के लिए 2017 में यूपी सरकार के कामकाज पूरा होने पर दो साल तक सर्वदलीय सरकार काम करे, फिर 2019 में चुनाव हो।

2. 2020 में 4 राज्यों में ही चुनाव है। उन्हें एक साल पहले कराया जाए।

3. 7 राज्यों में 2018 में चुनाव होने हैं। लोकसभा के साथ 12 राज्यों के चुनाव एक साल पहले करा लिए जाएं।
.

अगर किसी राज्य या केंद्र में किसी पार्टी को स्पष्ट बहुमत नहीं मिले तो प्रस्ताव किया जा रहा है कि :
1. सभी दल मिलकर मिलीजुली सरकार बनाए ।
2. सभी दल मिलकर अनिवार्य तौर पर सर्वदलीय सरकार बनाएं
3. या अगले चुनाव तक राष्ट्रपति शासन लगा रहे।
4. बीच में विधानसभा भंग होने पर भी सरकार चलाने की यही व्यवस्था लागू हो।
.

मतलब सरकार बनाने की कानूनी 'मजबूरी' के चलते कोंग्रेस बीजेपी सपा बसपा लेफ्ट आदि सभी दल मिलकर सरकार बना सकेंगे । जिसके पास जितने विधायक सांसद होंगे उन्हें उस प्रकार से सत्ता में भागीदारी दे दी जायेगी । जवाबदेही में गिरावट आएगी, एजेंडे खुले तौर पर गड्ड मड्ड हो जायेंगे, एक नयी प्रकार की जमात का जन्म होगा जो 5 वर्ष में एक बार जनता को शक्ल दिखाएँगे, और बाकी सब चीजे संसद में आपस में बैठ कर तय कर लेंगे ।
.

ज्ञातव्य है कि हाल ही में चुनाव आयोग ने सिफारिश की है कि, निर्दलीय उम्मीदवारों के चुनाव लड़ने को निर्बन्धित किया जाए, और अब ये क़ानून लाने के मंसूबे बांधे जा रहे है ।
जाहिर है, सभी राजनेतिक दल इस बात पर सहमत है, कि नये उम्मीदवारों और पार्टियों को चुनाव लड़ने से बेदखल किया जाए और यह सुनिश्चित किया जाए कि नागरिको की भागीदारी चुनावी प्रक्रिया में कम से कम रहे ।
.

समाधान यह है कि नागरिक राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों की मांग करे, ताकि राजनेतिक दलों में अपनी नौकरी खोने का भय बना रहे । राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों के होने से पार्टी व्यवस्था कमजोर हो जायेगी, जनप्रतिनिधियों में जवाबदेही बढ़ेगी, राजनेतिक दलों का एकाधिकार कमजोर होगा और सभी नेता कार्यकुशल हो जायेंगे ।

__________________

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Mar 22 2015 10:58:03 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

A key difference between China and India wrt recruitment of entry level junior officers / judges
.
1. The interviews have near zero weightage !! And it is taken by randomly selected panels so the interview-giver and interview-taker have no way to know who will take my interviews or give interviews to me. So interview fixing is less.
.
2. The recruitment tests do NOT test for any language at all !!
.
3. The recruitment tests also dont insist on qualifications that can be gained in 2-3 years of training. eg non-law graduates can appear in National Judicial Exams and can even get selected !!! The only thing is that non-law graduates, if they get selected , have to spend extra years than a law graduate
.
4. Class-XII education is more universal than in India. And so officers are chosen from a broader base.
.
5. Most recruitment exams test for some level of maths / logic . And there is emphasis to make basic science compulsory for all positions in govt !!
.
6. Communist Party also uses performances in exams for internal promotions at very junior levels !!
.
=====
.
At intermediate and senior levels, promotions are by discretion of apex officers.
.
=====
.
Due to all these factors, average merit of Chinese judges is far far higher than average merit of judges in India, who are recruited exclusively by nepotism. And average of merit of officer in China is same as officer in India as both come by exams, but recruitment exams still use interview and so are ridden by nepotism. And no political party in India use examination score for internal promotion !! And so merit of Chinese politicians is better than Indian politicians in general.
.
=====
.
Recruitment in USA
================
.
In USA , recruitment at base level is via exams , but most nodal positions are elected or based on discretion of apex leaders. eg Police Chiefs, Public Prosecutors, District Judges, Mayor, Governorrs, President etc are elected . The elected persons are recallable except Federal levels.
.
And so generally these elected men (except Senators) are less corrupt and are pressed to work efficiently. So they hire best of the technical talent that their budgets can buy and emphasis is on efficiencies and less on favor.
.
So person at nodal position in USA may not always be very bright , but he does ensure that technical persons he has chosen are indeed bright , hard working and smart working. And a large number of officers get replaced each time Governor or President changes. eg when President changes, some 8000 officers will change. They are often called as political appointees, who are appointed by President, with or without Senate Conformation. (see page-200 of Unites States Plum Book at http://www.gpo.gov/fdsys/pkg/GPO-PLUMBOOK-2012/pdf/GPO-PLUMBOOK-2012.pdf )
.
=============
.
All in all, we have quite bad recruitment policies . We use interviews at entry level positions which ensures rampant corruption and rampant nepotism. The elected leaders arent recallable and so qorst performance is guaranteed. The courts dont have Jury System , so nexuses between lawyers and judges rule the courts !!!
.
Unless we fix all these things, I dont think we can stop the march of MNC-owners Missionaries in India.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Mar 22 2015 17:05:46 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

My original discovery in field of History is ---- IMO re-emergence of Europe after 1200 AD was due to re-installation of Jury System, and "Renaissance" is just a fairy tale !!
.
No historoian till date has prfaised Jury System as "root cause" behind strength of Greece , Romans and Modern Europe. So right or wrong, this claim of mine is --- ORIGINAL !!
.
====
.
Historians say "Renaissance" is what made modern Europe strong. Renaissance is the sum total of changes in Europe that happened between 1200 AD and 1400 AD . These changes were ONLY effects or symptoms. They were NOT causes of any progress --- they are just label to progress.
.
Jury System was main reason why Greek and later Rome became very powerful. And Jury System collapsed in around 100 AD.
.
And Jury System restarted in 950 AD and 1100 AD in UK and started spreading across Western Europe.
.
Due to Jury System, the power of officers and elitemen to harass small craftsmen and small factory owners reduced. And Jury System , manufacturing improved , economy improved, trade improved , and weapon making improved.
.
What is more is that Jury System created a system of "reasonable rate of interest" !! Before that, interest rate was sky high or zero. The first one robs craftsman and stops technical progress in society. And lumpsum ban on interest leaves only one option --- partnership which doesnt scale to large enterprise !! This further improved manufacturing and weapons.
.
Trendline in 900 AD was ---- Arabs / Turks etc were marching on and on and on without nothing to stop them. The reason behind their march was ---- the procedure that "everyone in locality must gather on one day a week at one place" !!!!! The fact that ALL have to gather reduces the ability of strong to exploit the weak, because his exploitation will get exposed in front of all !!! So procedure that "all must gather at one place on one day a week" reduces exploitation of weak by strong !!
.
But Jury System is more effective than gathering (WHY?). And West also copied concept of "every one gather at one place" at town level , and later extended it at national level using booth based elections.
.
All in all, world is now better place to live ONLY because Jury System came back !!! Otherwise, things would have been same as 900 AD , and its trendline.
.
The IMO moral for today is ----- we in India bring Jury System ASAP, Othewise, IMO, we are doomed. There is no way we can match the strength that Jury System gives to the society, manufacturing, economy and Military.
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sun Mar 22 2015 19:24:57 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

विडम्बना !!!

विहिप और आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता सुबह उन बीजेपी नेताओं की जय जयकार करते है, जो कि हिन्दू धर्म को कमजोर करके मिशनरीज को धर्मान्तर में सहयोग करते है, और जब मिशनरीज़ हिन्दुओ का धर्मान्तर करती है, तो शाम को विहिप और आरएसएस के कार्यकर्ता मिशनरीज़ के खिलाफ नारे लगाते है, उपद्रव मचाते है और हंगामा खड़ा करते है । किन्तु विहिप और आरएसएस द्वारा सुबह किया गया सहयोग मिशनरीज को धर्मान्तर में ज्यादा सहायता देता है, जबकि शाम को किया गया तमाशा निष्फल हो जाता है !!
.

कुछ उदाहरणों से इसे समझते है ।

===========

सुबह विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता बीजेपी, कोंग्रेस, आम पार्टी के इस फैसले का समर्थन करते है कि खनन और प्राकृतिक संसाधनों से प्राप्त रोयल्टी को सीधे नागरिको के खाते में जमा नही किया जाना चाहिए ।

नतीजा : गरीबी और भुखमरी बनी रहती है ।
.
दिन में मिशनरीज़ गरीब व्यक्ति को लालच देकर धर्मान्तर करती है, और शाम को विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता चिल्लाते है, कि देखो मिशनरीज हिन्दुओ का धर्मान्तर कर रही है ।

============

सुबह विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता बीजेपी कोंग्रेस और आम पार्टी के इस फैसले का समर्थन करती है कि खनन से प्राप्त रोयल्टी नागरिको के खाते में जमा नही की जानी चाहिए ।

नतीजा : सीनियर सिटिज़न को पेंशन नही मिल पाती और उन्हें आश्रित जीवन जीना पड़ता है, जबकि मिशनरीज़ वृद्धाश्रम आदि अच्छे से संचालित करती है, जिसे वृद्ध व्यक्तियों को सेवा सुसुश्रा के लिए पुत्र की आवश्यकता महसूस नही होती । इससे ईसाईयों में लिंगानुपात सम बना रहता है, जबकि हिन्दुओ में पुरषो के मुकाबले स्त्री अनुपात गिरता है ।
.
दोपहर को ईसाई लड़की से शादी करने के लिए हिन्दू युवक पादरी के सहयोग से धर्मान्तर करता है, और शाम को विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता नारे लगाते है, कि मिशनरीज़ धर्मान्तर कर रहे है ।

============

सुबह विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता बीजेपी, कोंग्रेस और आम पार्टी के साथ खड़े होकर राईट टू रिकाल एजुकेशन ऑफिसर के क़ानून का विरोध करते है, जिससे सरकारी स्कूल बदहाल बने रहते है, और गरीब तबके को शिक्षा नही मिल पाती ।
.
नतीजा : मिशनरीज अपने विपुल कोष से स्कूल्स चलाते है, और उन लोगो को शिक्षा मुहैया कराते है जिनकी हैसियत निजी स्कूलों की भारी भरकम फ़ीस अदा करने की नही है ।
.
दिन में मिशनरीज़ मुफ्त शिक्षा देकर धर्मान्तर करती है, और शाम को विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता हल्ला गुल्ला मचाते है कि, मिशनरीज़ धर्मान्तर कर रही है ।

============

सुबह विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता बीजेपी, कोंग्रेस और आम पार्टी के मंदिरों को सरकारी नियंत्रण में बनाए रखने के फैसले का समर्थन करते है, जिससे मंदिरों की संपत्ति पर सरकार कब्ज़ा लेती है और मंदिर स्कूल, अस्पताल, वृद्धाश्रम आदि का सञ्चालन नही कर पाते।
.
नतीजा : बेहद गरीब तबका मूलभूत आवश्यकताओं से वंचित बना रहता है ।
.
अपने सुप्रबंधित स्कूल्स और अस्पताल के बूते मिशनरीज़ दिन में धर्मान्तर करते है, और विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता शाम को गुल गपाड़ा मचाते है, कि मिशनरीज धर्मान्तर कर रही है ।

============

सुबह विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता बीजेपी, कोंग्रेस और आम पार्टी की FDI नीति का समर्थन करते है, जिससे MNCs के CSR में वृद्धि होती है और रोजगार के अवसर पैदा होते है ।
.
नतीजा : CSR से मिशनरीज को स्कूल, अस्पताल और वृद्ध्आश्रम चलाने के लिए दान मिलता है, और कम्पनियों में नौकरियों के अवसर बढ़ते है ।
.
दिन में मिशनरीज़ नौकरी और सुविधाओं का लालच देकर हिन्दुओ का धर्मान्तर करती है, और शाम को विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता मिशनरीज के खिलाफ ऐलान चलाते है, कि मिशनरीज धर्मान्तर कर रही है ।

=============

विडम्बना यह है कि, अगली सुबह विहिप आरएसएस कार्यकर्ता फिर से उन्ही बीजेपी नेताओं की पूजा करते है, जो लगातार ऐसी नीतिया बना रहे है, जिससे सरकारी स्कूल तबाह हो जाए, सरकारी अस्पताल बदहाल बने रहे, मंदिर सरकारों के कब्जे में बने रहे और सारा विकास FDI से ही हो ।

=====

इस सर्कस को आप कितनी गंभीरता से लेते है, यह आप पर निर्भर है ।

मैं अमेरिकन बनना चाहूँगा..
जिसका लोहा सारी दुनिया मानती है
.
मैं ब्रिटिश बनना चाहूँगा...
जिसकी भाषा को सारी दुनिया ने अपनाया है
.
मैं फ्रांसीसी बनना चाहूँगा...
फैशन, कला और वाइन का पारखी
.
मैं जर्मन बनना चाहूंगा...
पेशेवर, धुन का पक्का और मेहनती
.
मैं इटेलियन बनकर दुनिया को स्पोर्ट्स कार बनाना सिखाऊंगा
.
मै ब्राजिलियन बनना चाहूँगा...
फ़ुटबाल का चैम्पियन
.
मैं स्विस बनकर दुनिया को बेंकिंग सिखाऊंगा
.
मैं इस्राएली बनकर दुनिया को बताऊंगा कि लगन और हिम्मत के बल पर क्या किया जा सकता है
.
मै चीनी बनकर दुनिया का हमकदम बनना चाहूँगा
.
मै हिन्दुस्तानी बनना चाहूंगा...
मैं अपने देश पर गर्व करना चाहूंगा..
मैं चाहूंगा कि लोग यह सुनते ही जलने लगे कि मैं हिन्दुस्तानी हूँ ।
.

ऐसा हो सकता है..
ऐसा हो सकता है, अगर हम यह बात समझ लें कि :

अच्छे कानूनों से ही अच्छा देश बनाया जा सकता है ।
क्योंकि देश को क़ानून चलाते है, क्योंकी सिर्फ कानूनों से ही देश में अच्छे परिवर्तन लाये जा सकते है, क्योंकि ये क़ानून ही है जो किसी देश को अच्छा या बुरा बना देते है, क्योंकि सरकारों का काम सिर्फ क़ानून बनाना है भासण देना नही, क्योंकि जिस देश की जनता कानूनों में रुचि नही लेती वह देश या तो गुलाम बन जाता है या भिखारी, क्योंकि विकसित कानूनों से ही देश विकसित हुए है, क्योंकि भारत इसीलिए पिछड़ा हुआ है, क्योंकि भारत में बुरे और पिछड़े हुए क़ानून है, क्योंकि राजनीति शब्द में नीति शब्द का मतलब क़ानून है, क्योंकि सारे देश एक जैसे हो जायेंगे यदि सभी देशो में एक जैसे क़ानून लागू कर दिए जाए, क्योंकि हमें यदि अमेरिका और ब्रिटेन से मजबूत देश बनाना है, तो उनसे अच्छे क़ानून लागू करने होंगे, क्योंकि सभी देशो में उतने ही घटिया नेता है जितने कि भारत में है, क्योंकि अच्छे क़ानून ही अच्छे नागरिको की रचना करते है, क्योंकि अच्छे क़ानून ही अच्छे नेताओ को बढावा दे सकते है, क्योंकि राजनीति में हर समस्या का समाधान सिर्फ कानून से ही निकल सकता है ।
.

भारत भी उतना ही विकसित हो सकता है, जितनी की अन्य महाशक्तियां है, बशर्ते हम नेताओं की भक्ति करने के स्थान पर अच्छे कानूनों की मांग करे ।

Dear recallists,
.
Please google on "athravaved sabha" and also on ""rigved sabha" .
.
It turns out that TCP is perhaps the LEAST inefficient way of implementing sabha for large matters !!! And Jury is effeicient way of implementing sabha for small cases !!!
.
And those who want to solve more difficult logic problems --- please also see
.
=====
.
http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Common_knowledge_(logic)
.
"On an island, there are k people who have blue eyes, and the rest of the people have green eyes. At the start of the puzzle, no one on the island ever knows their own eye color. By rule, if a person on the island ever discovers they have blue eyes, that person must leave the island at dawn the next day. On the island, each person knows every other person's eye color, there are no reflective surfaces, and there is no discussion of eye color."
.
At some point, an outsider comes to the island, calls together all the people on the island, and makes the following public announcement: "At least one of you has blue eyes". The outsider, furthermore, is known by all to be truthful, and all know that all know this, and so on: it is common knowledge that he is truthful, and thus it becomes common knowledge that there is at least one islander who has blue eyes. The problem: assuming all persons on the island are completely logical and that this too is common knowledge, what is the eventual outcome?
.
======
.
For more, see the wiki link and pls google on "common knowledge wiki".
.
It turns out that open discussion are NOT sufficient to fix certain wrongs, and ONLY open discussion in "sabha" can fix certain wrongs !!!
.
Common knowledge is not just "everyone knows". It is "everyone knows that everyone knows that everyone knows that everyone knows ...." to infinity !!! And without "sabha", common knowledge cant be extended and will become static.
.
"Sabha" in common words mean --- everyone must gather at one place on one day one every week or once every month. Sabha ensures that whatever is spoken in sabha " everyone knows", and "everyone knows that everyone knows that everyone knows ...." infinity. It is higher than sending instruction on a pamphlets , because this ONLY ensures that "everyone knows" and doesnt ensure that "everyone knows that everyone knows".
.
But sabha has two problems.
.
(1) when number of persons > 500 or 1000 , physically gathering all at one place, and ONE TIME is increasingly difficult.
.
(2) if all decisions are taken by sabha, and if 100 cases are to be resolved by sabha, then sabha cant give enough time to both parties in all 100 cases to present their sides.
.
====
.
TCP reduces first problem
.
Jury System reduces second problem.
.
====
.
For more, please search on how different coimmunites and legal systems implemented concept of sabha.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Mar 23 2015 12:38:54 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How Go-Raksha reduced / eliminated slavery in ancient India --- bullocks provided plentiful cheaper labor
.
India was first country in world to eliminate slavery. And one reason was --- bullock labor became plentiful due to go-raksha that was susdized by rich via donations.
.
In ancient Greece \ Rome , some 60% to 80% population was slave and slavery lasted till 300 AD. Arab / Turk / Moguls had slavery for long long time. China too had slavery till 1600 AD. But slavery ended in ancient India loooong back - way before villages started. It appears that large scale slavery never came in Indian sub-continent. One reason was go-raksha which was subsidized by donations from rich.
.
Agriculture required huge amount of labor. And slave is intelligent than a bullock. And a slave is fed by his parents till he is 14 to 16 years old, and then he is enslaved in slave raids and then sold. All this makes a slave cheaper than bullock !! Which is why most societies outside India preferred to keep bullocks for meat rather then use them for labor.
.
But in India, go-pooja and related "daan" subsidized cows as well as bullocks. And slaughter became sinful. So bullocks became plentiful. And so there was almost no need for human slaves !!
.
.=========================
.
The lack of slavery played IMPORTANT role in protecting Hinduism from Arabs etc. How?
.
Please see Arab conquests from Arab to eastwards till Sindhu river and westward -- whole northern africa, middle africa, and also along Turkey , middle east etc. Here Arabs / Turks etc sailed on with ease. The real resistance came only near Sindhu river and when they tried to cross into Europe from Turkey or from Northen Africa. IOW, except Catholics and Hindus, others fell like house of cards. WHY?
.
Because Arabs offered freedom to slaves if they accepted Islam. So places like Iraq, Iran etc which back then had huge slave population started falling like house of cards because their "own" slaves would side with Arab invaders. Whereas in Europe and India, there wasnt much slave population to speak of. So a big benefit that Arabs etc got in wars in then Iraq, Iran, central asia, africa was NOT available in India and Europe.
.
It is interesting to see how subsidies , via daan, for cows and bullocks and very concept of goraksha had such far far reaching effect. It reduced slavery to near zero if not absolute zero, and thus strengthened society.
.
===============
.
Today, cows / bullocks are needed more than past !! Thats because urine is needed is huge quantity for pesticide. It is now proven that chemical pesticide cause caner and GM crops which have toxins built into plants are worse. The only remedy left to kill pests etc is animal urine. Further , urine has bacteria which improve soil,. While chemical pesticides not dont have such bacteria, but the kill such bacteria as well. And likewise animal dung is better fertilizer than chemical fertilier because animal dung has bacteria which improve soil, while chemical fertilizer doesnt have these bacteria kills those baacteria.
.
Using bullock instead of tractor too has merits because India is fuel deficient and we import 65% or more of the crude / gas we consume. Also , tractors hardens the ground and reduce fertility. And tractors emit more carbon dioxide . fumes etc then bullocks.
.
All in all, goraksha and traditions to donate for cows reduced slavery in ancient India to zero. And today too, it can serve 10s of purposes.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Mar 23 2015 14:33:15 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

My second discovery in History is ----- not many fought in the name of God ; the fights were for REAL issues such as
.
(1) who decides which persons will become "judges" (judges = those decide facts, criminality and length of punishment)
.
(2) who will decide tenure of judges --- lifetime or fixed number of years?
.
(3) should interest on loan be permitted or banned !!! .
.
(4) Inheritance , family laws
.
And so forth,
.
IOW, people of different "religions" fought for these REAL issues --- not just for sake of God and beliefs about God.
.
===============
.
Since childhood, I wondered --- can so many men who otherwise so rational that they can make magnificent marvels in engineering , medicine etc be so irrational that they will fight only in the name of God ? ".
.
To me, it looked like "rational in morning and irrational in afternoon" , and ddidnt addup.
.
After many many readings, my conclusion is that fights between communities were for REAL physical material reasons which do effect day to day lives of all humans, I have mentioned some of the physical reasons above. There are many more.
.
A key question is "who decides who will decide guilt". Please note that this question is more important that "who will decide guilt" and also more important that "what is crime"? Because finally , what is crime and what is not crime is NOT decided by written law, because written law can alway be twisted like a piece of wax by the person who is "interpreting" and "applying the law on the case". And before law is applied, the fact has to be decided by humans, and the human who is deciding facts in effect decides the case.
.
So who will decide guilt is important. And even if society of million managed to find one saintly soul, the question is only 1% solved because that one lone saintly soul cant decide 1000s of cases coming every year. So that soul will APPOINT junior officers / judges / sabha / jurors etc
.
So the "appointment procedures to decide WHICH PERSONS will decide guilt" are important, because finally the punishment will be decided by thee men who decide the guilt/punishments ".
.
And there were real reasons as well such as --- should interest-bearing loans be allowed or not.
.
So here are key differences between religions etc. And these differences are real.
.
1. Mohammad created the tradition in Islam that "everyone must gather at one place aka mosque at least once a day aka on Friday". The "gathering of all" is powerful tool to make things public. And many disputes are decided in this gathering by Maulavi. But Maulavi cant decided the dispute in private. He must give ruling in gathering before all, which acts as a check on him. The judge i.e. Maulavi isnt inherited or self-appointed position. The maulvi is chosen by town elders who manage the mosque. And the elders do NOT have permanent tenure. A set of elders is replaced by another set of respected elders every few years.
.
2. Not so in Hindus. The different sects have different traditions. But except Sikhs and Arya Samaj, temples are almost private properties !! And there is no "gathering" i.e. everyone must gather at one place on one specific day in a week. And since ages , temples, except in Sikhs, reduced public work as well as giving rulings on disputes. The punishments are decided solely by Panch or "learned men" appointed by Kings, and Kings are hereditary !!! IOW, temples as well as administration are heriditary or "learned men" appointed by previous set of "learned men". And appointments are life time.
.
3. The priests in Catholics used to do many tasks which Maulvis do - such as deciding punishments , resolving disputes , keeping records of marriages / births / deaths etc . But the priests are appointed by priests' heirachy and NOT by town elders.
.
IOW, each "religion" has a court structure at local level which is incompatible with court structure of other. IOW, the fight want not just for beliefs but which court structure should be prevail.
.
Likewise, interest on loans was a real issue. Back then , gold was currency. And gold cant be created like today's money by fiat. So even 5% per YEAR interest on gold loans is sufficient to create extreme concentration of wealth and cause bankruptcy. And back then, bankruptcies meant slavery of borrower , sometimes slavery of family members of borrowers as well, and sometimes borrower's sons have to toil for generations to pay back the debt.
.
Here, different "religions" took different stand
.
1. Hinduism and Judaism have no rules against limit on interest rates.
.
2. Catholics / Muslims banned even 0,001% interest rate !!
.
So if a Jew or Hindu money lender becomes Muslims, he loses his income from interest. And if Hindu borrower becomes Muslim or Catholic, then he gets partial or whole relief from debt !! Whether interest on loans is good or bad is another topic. But what I want to show is that "its NOT just God, but something real" which playing role. .
.
Likewise, the fight between Protestants and Catholics had some real issue. In Protestants, the priest is appointed NOT by central hierachy but by church trustees, and church is community property and trustees are twon elders or community elders. And trustees arent life time and change every few years and there is no inheritance and life long appointment.
.
And in protestants in UK, Germany etc, the disputes were to be resolved by not by Priests by but Jurors or officers of kings. The priests confined to spiritual world only.
.
IOW, the clash was due to REAL reasons, not just their differences on interpretation of religions.
.
All in all, I have made a list of 10s of REAL issues , like appointment of judges, tenure of judges, powers of judges, interest on loans etc and NOT mere abstract issues like God , which became causes of serious clashes.
.
IOW , rational men fought WW1 for iron and coal, and fought WW2 for crude oil, iron and coal in that order, and not for religions. And men in past also fought wars for REAL RATIONAL factors like appointment / tenure of judges and interest on loans. Good or bad, world was never an irrational place.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Mar 23 2015 15:47:40 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How even interest rate as low as 1% per year on GOLD loan can cause extreme concentration of wealth OR large number of bankruptcies OR both !!
.
Lets consider an island of 10000 persons. Say each gold coin is 1 gram.
.
Say wealth men have 10 times gold then non-wealthy.
.
Say 100 wealthy men have 100 kg of gold each , and remaining 9900 men have 1 kg each.
,
i.e.
number of persons on island = 10000
one coin = 1 gram
number of wealthy persons on island = 100
number of non-wealthy persons on island = 9900
gold each wealthy person has = 100 kg
total gold owned by wealthy = 10,000 kg
gold each non-wealthy person has = 1 kg
gold owned by all non-wealthy persons = 1 kg * 9900 = 9900 kg
.
Total gold on island = 10000 kg + 9900 kg = 19,900 kg
.
Now say wealth individuals lend 90% of gold at just 1% interest per year. Say there are NO bankrupties and all were able to repay the loans.
.
Then at the end of the year,
.
amount of gold with wealthy persons = 10100 kg
amount of gold with non-wealthy persons = 9800 kg
.
Because new gold doesnt fall from sky .
.
So if there are NO bankruptcies, then gold owned by wealth individuals will grow at 1% per year and gold owned by non-wealthy will reduce by same amount. So at end of nth year,
.
amount of gold with wealthy persons = 10000 * (1.01)^N
amount of gold with non-wealthy persons = 9900 - addition in above
.
So in some 72 years, all gold will be owned by wealthy individuals only !!
.
And if interest rate is 5% a year, then bottom 99% will become penniless in mere 14 - 15 years !!
.
And if interest rate is 10% a year, then bottom 99% will become penniless in mere 7 - 8 years !!
.
Today, we dont see massive bankruptcies even with 9% to 12% interest on loans charged by banks. Thats because, today, RBI manufactured new money at the rate of 15% a year !!! i.e. today, money does fall from sky. But gold doesnt fall from sky.
.
So just 1% interest on gold can make whole society penniless in just 70 years !! And interest of 2% to 3% per MONTH i.e. 24% per year on gold loans used to be common !! This can make society penniless in
.
So bankrupticies are imminent.
.
Now in ancient days, bankruptcies meant that borrower has to become slave of lenader !! And sometimes, his family has to become slave as well !! And in India, where slavery ended looong back due to goraksha , debt meant that children will have to work provide cheap labor till debt is paid which means for generations !!!
.
=-====
.
This was a principal reason why Christianity / Islam banned even 0.01% interest on loans.
.
Alternative was partnership. But one can have 10-20 partners. So large ventures become difficult to create and manage.
.
The problem reduced only after Jury System came , where Jurors (1) started allowing interest (2) punishing lenders who charged excessive interest rate (3) ensured that bakruptcies do not cause slavery not long term binding. So countries which started practising Jury System got "non-repressive reasonable rate of interest on loans" and so commerce and industries n those countries grew at much faster rate then rest of the world.
.
But all in all, absence of Jury System , the "rate of interest on loans" concept alone is sufficient to ruin bottom 95% of the population. And we are seeing that across the world from USA to India, where Jury System is weakening or doesnt exist.

Mon Mar 23 2015 15:59:16 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How section-138 of Negotiable Instrument in India is being used by lenders to rob borrowers !!
.
section-138 of Negotiable Instrument Act says "if X writes a cheque to Y and cheque bounces and Y complains, then X will be imprisoned for upto 1 years or even upto 2 years !! "
.
Now what lenders do is --- when they loan say Rs 1 lakh, then ask borrower to give a cheque of Rs 100,000 or sometimes twice , with NO DATE ON IT !!
/.
And when borrower fails to repay, the lender writes current date and deposits it and then drags the borrower to the court !!
.
And threat of prison is used for repayment of loan or more amount
.
=====
.
Solution I propose is
.
1. The cheques should have expiry date of 90 dayes after chequebook was issued by bank
.
2. Only CURRENT date should be put on the cheque. i.e. if X gives cheque to Y , then X must put current date and if cheque is undated, then Y must returns it , but Y cannot put date on cheque. Also, if cheque is post dated even by 1 day, then Y must return it.
.
3. Jurors should decide if the cheque given was undated or post dated.
.
IOW, section-138 NE-act should be applied ONLY on current-dated or pre-dated cheques and NOT on undated or post-dated cheques.
.
Otherwise, this "imprisonment on failure to pay debt" will create huge poverty and will only benefit naxals and missionaries.
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Mon Mar 23 2015 16:30:38 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

स्थान : प्रधानमन्त्री नरेन्द्र मोदी का शयन कक्ष
दिन : 23 मार्च 2015
समय : 1 am
.

मोदी साहेब : (चौंकते हुए ) कौन.. ? कौन है वहां ?

आवाज : मैं हूँ.. भगत
.

मोदी साहेब : कौन भगत ??

आवाज : मैं भगत सिंह संधू , पंजाब से, जिसे 23 मार्च 1931 को फांसी दी गयी थी ।
.
मोदी साहेब : एं...आप यहाँ । मेरा प्रणाम स्वीकार करिए । बीजेपी शहीदों का बहुत सम्मान करती है । मै स्वयं सदा से ही क्रांतिकारियों के ....

भगत : ठीक है ठीक है.. । मैं एक विशेष प्रयोजन से आपके समक्ष उपस्थित हुआ हूँ ।

मोदी साहेब : आप आदेश करिए, हम आपकी इच्छा के लिए अपना..

भगत : अच्छी बात है । मैं चाहता हूँ कि आप मेरी उस मांग को स्वीकार करें, जो मैं ब्रिटिश शासन से भी कर रहा था, और आज भी कर रहा हूँ ।
.
मोदी साहेब : ऐसी कौनसी मांग है, जो इतने वर्षो से लंबित है.. !! ओह, कहीं आप राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों के लिए तो नहीं कह रहे है ?

भगत : बिलकुल, हमने 1925 में HSRA का मेनिफेस्टो प्रकाशित किया था, जिसमें हमने इन कानूनों की मांग की थी ।
.
मोदी साहेब : देखिये भगत जी, ऐसे क़ानून गेजेट में प्रकाशित करना मेरे बस की बात नही । सारे सांसद, विधायक, धनिक, नौकरशाही आदि सभी मेरे खिलाफ हो जायेंगे और मुझे कुर्सी से हाथ धोना पड़ेगा । आप समझदार व्यक्ति है, सब समझते है । मैं एक अदना सा नेता हूँ, क्रांतिकारी नहीं ।
.
भगत : मैं जानता हूँ ऐसे क़ानून जनदबाव से ही लाये जा सकते है, और देश में कुछ मुट्ठी भर नागरिक आज भी यह जानकारी फैलाने का कार्य कर रहे है, किन्तु आप के बारे में कुछ और ही सुना था इसीलिए टटोलने चला आया ।
.
मोदी साहेब : नही नही, आप मुझे गलत समझ रहे है, मैं स्वयं राईट टू रिकाल में मानता हूँ, किन्तु..

भगत : ठीक है, तो कम से कम इतना कीजिये कि मुझे श्रन्धांजलि देने की जगह, यह बयान जारी कीजिये कि भगत सिंह, चंद्रशेखर आजाद और सान्याल जी ने राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों की मांग की थी । ताकि यह जानकारी करोड़ो नागरिको तक पहुंचे कि हमने अपनी जान किस मिशन के लिए कुर्बान की थी ।
.
मोदी साहेब : माफ़ कीजिये, लेकिन ऐसा करने से तो अगले ही दिन आप में मानने वाले सभी लोग इन कानूनों की मांग करने लगेंगे...
आप कहें तो मैं जंतर मंतर पर आपकी सरदार पटेल से भी ऊँची प्रतिमा लगवां दूं ?

भगत : आपको मेरे समर्थन का स्वांग भरने की कोई आवश्यकता नही । मैं उस ब्रिटिश शासन को भी समर्थन देने से नही हिचकूंगा जो कि राईट टू रिकाल से युक्त हो, और उस स्वराज्य का भी विरोध करता हूँ जो जिसमे नागरिको के पास अपने प्रतिनिधियों को वापिस बुलाने का अधिकार न हो । मुझे राईट टू रिकाल विहीन स्वराज, लोकतंत्र या आजादी कुछ भी मंज़ूर नहीं । मालूम हुआ, आप भी उन व्यक्तियों की श्रेणी में है जो जाहिर में मेरी शहादत पर आँसू बहाते है, किन्तु मेरे सिद्धांतो की हत्या करके अपना मंतव्य सिद्ध करते है ।
चलता हूँ.. और हाँ, इस फेर में न रहिएगा कि आप की बिरादरी के किये ही सब कुछ होगा... देश में कई भगत और उधम आज भी विचरते है ।
.

a tribute to mahatma bhagat, mahatma rajguru and mahatma sukhdev.
