Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate:

How Go-Raksha reduced / eliminated slavery in ancient India --- bullocks provided plentiful cheaper labor
.
India was first country in world to eliminate slavery. And one reason was --- bullock labor became plentiful due to go-raksha that was susdized by rich via donations.
.
In ancient Greece \ Rome , some 60% to 80% population was slave and slavery lasted till 300 AD. Arab / Turk / Moguls had slavery for long long time. China too had slavery till 1600 AD. But slavery ended in ancient India loooong back - way before villages started. It appears that large scale slavery never came in Indian sub-continent. One reason was go-raksha which was subsidized by donations from rich.
.
Agriculture required huge amount of labor. And slave is intelligent than a bullock. And a slave is fed by his parents till he is 14 to 16 years old, and then he is enslaved in slave raids and then sold. All this makes a slave cheaper than bullock !! Which is why most societies outside India preferred to keep bullocks for meat rather then use them for labor.
.
But in India, go-pooja and related "daan" subsidized cows as well as bullocks. And slaughter became sinful. So bullocks became plentiful. And so there was almost no need for human slaves !!
.
.=========================
.
The lack of slavery played IMPORTANT role in protecting Hinduism from Arabs etc. How?
.
Please see Arab conquests from Arab to eastwards till Sindhu river and westward -- whole northern africa, middle africa, and also along Turkey , middle east etc. Here Arabs / Turks etc sailed on with ease. The real resistance came only near Sindhu river and when they tried to cross into Europe from Turkey or from Northen Africa. IOW, except Catholics and Hindus, others fell like house of cards. WHY?
.
Because Arabs offered freedom to slaves if they accepted Islam. So places like Iraq, Iran etc which back then had huge slave population started falling like house of cards because their "own" slaves would side with Arab invaders. Whereas in Europe and India, there wasnt much slave population to speak of. So a big benefit that Arabs etc got in wars in then Iraq, Iran, central asia, africa was NOT available in India and Europe.
.
It is interesting to see how subsidies , via daan, for cows and bullocks and very concept of goraksha had such far far reaching effect. It reduced slavery to near zero if not absolute zero, and thus strengthened society.
.
===============
.
Today, cows / bullocks are needed more than past !! Thats because urine is needed is huge quantity for pesticide. It is now proven that chemical pesticide cause caner and GM crops which have toxins built into plants are worse. The only remedy left to kill pests etc is animal urine. Further , urine has bacteria which improve soil,. While chemical pesticides not dont have such bacteria, but the kill such bacteria as well. And likewise animal dung is better fertilizer than chemical fertilier because animal dung has bacteria which improve soil, while chemical fertilizer doesnt have these bacteria kills those baacteria.
.
Using bullock instead of tractor too has merits because India is fuel deficient and we import 65% or more of the crude / gas we consume. Also , tractors hardens the ground and reduce fertility. And tractors emit more carbon dioxide . fumes etc then bullocks.
.
All in all, goraksha and traditions to donate for cows reduced slavery in ancient India to zero. And today too, it can serve 10s of purposes.

Question :- Right To Recall /वापस बुलाने का अधिकार " ये कानून कुछ यूरेपिन देशो मे है स्विटजरलैंड आदि मे ! वहां पे संख्या निश्चित है कि इतने लोग एक स्थान पे इकटठे हों और किसी भी जन प्रतिनिधि को एक प्रक्रिया के तहत वापस बुला सकते हैं ! लेकिन हमारे देश मे संसदिय क्षेत्र काफी बडे होते हैं तो ऐसे मे हमारे देश मे इस अधिकार का प्रयोग करने की प्रक्रिया कैसी होगी क्या आप इस बारै मे कुछ बताने का कष्ट करेंगे??

Answer:- पहली बात तो ये विचारणीय है कि संसदीय क्षेत्र अगर बडे हैं तो चुनावी मतदान के समय लोग कैसे एकत्र हो जाते हैं?

दूसरी बात ये कि क्या चंद्रशेखर आज़ाद इत्यादि ने अपने हिंदुस्तान रिपब्लिकर एसोसिएशन के दिसम्बर १९२४ मैनिफ़ेस्टो में स्पष्ट रूप से लिखा है कि "जिस गणराज्य की स्थापना हम करने वाले हैं वहां मतदाताओ के पास राईट टू रिकॉल होगा अन्यथा वह गणराज्य सिर्फ़ गणराज्य के नाम पर एक म्ज़ाक होगा"

और तीसरी बात ये कि राईट टू रिकॉल ग्रूप ने राईट टू रिकॉल की जो प्रक्रिया सुझाई है उसमे एक दिन सभी मतदाताॐ को एकत्रित होने की कोई आवश्यकता नहीं है। जो भी उम्मीदवार मंत्री पद प्राप्त करना चाहते हैं वो अपना नाम कैबिनेट सेक्रेटरी के पास जाकर एफ़िडेविट देकर दर्ज करवा देंगे और भारत का कोई भी मतदाता किसी भी दिन पटवारी के दफ़्तर जाकर अपना मत किसी भी उम्मीदवार के पक्ष मे बदल सक्ता है और कम्प्यूट्अर पर उसका मत दर्ज होगा और उसे रसीद मिलेगी अथवा बहुत से लोग अपने वोटर आई डी के साथ रजिस्टर्ड मोबाईल नम्बर से एस एम एस भेज कर वोट दे देंगे और उन्हे सबूत के तौर पर एक फ़ीडबैक मेसेज मिलेगा। और जिस दिन किसी दूसरे उम्मीदवार को ५१% वोटरों के वोट मिल गये उस दिन वर्तमान मंत्री पद पर आसीन व्यक्ति की जगह पर दूसरा वाला उम्मीदवार बैठ जाएगा। उसी तरह प्रधानमंत्री का रिकॉल तब होगा जब किसी भी उम्मीदवार के हिस्से मे वर्तमान उम्मीदवार से २% अधिक वोट मिल गये यानि कि मान लीजिए कि २२ लोगों ने वरतमान मे किसी प्रधाअनमंत्री के लिये वोट किया ह और बाद मे २२ लोगों मे से १२ लोगओ ने किसी दूसरे को वोट किया तो १० की तुलना मे १२ यानि कि वर्तमान प्रधानम्न्त्री से २% अधिक किसी अन्य को मिला तो वो आदमी पी एम बनेगा। इसी से मिलती जुलती प्रक्रिया सांसद एवं विधायक पुलिस कमिश्नर , जज इत्यादि के रिकॉल की भी होगी

Questions : please post answers on your walls , and not here. Comments posted will be deleted. In comment box, pls ONLY post link to status on your wall which answers the question.
.
.
Aristotle said that if one asks questions on ethics, politics, law etc, he must answer it. So I will answer my question.
.
Say you are in Jury. Then how many months of prison sentence would YOU impose in each case. 0 months means = no imprisonment = acquittal
.
1. B = Borrower has borrowed Rs 1,00,000 from L = Lender on say 1-feb-2014 . And he had promised to return in writing on 1-feb-2015 i.e. 12 months later along with say 15% per year interest. And all search show that B has no money to pay back. L demands prison sentence of upto 24 months.
.
And say you are in Jury. Then how many months of prison sentence would you impose? (0 --- means no prison sentence = acquittal )
.
My answer --- 0
.
=====
.
2. B = Borrower has borrowed Rs 1,00,000 from L = Lender on say 1-feb-2014 . And he had promised in writing to return the money on 1-feb-2015 i.e. 12 months later along with say 15% per year interest.
.
And B had given a cheque of Rs 115,000 to L. B had also written amount and date on the cheque as Rs 115,000/- and date as 1-feb-2015.
.
The cheque bounces. And all search show that B has no money to pay L . And say you are in Jury. Then how many months of prison sentence would you impose? (0 --- means no prison sentence = acquittal )
.
My answer --- 0 months
.
===============
.
2. B = Borrower has borrowed Rs 100,000 from L = Lender on say 1-feb-2014 . And B had promised to return in writing on 1-feb-2015 i.e. 12 months later along with say 15% per year interest.
.
And B had given a cheque of Rs 115,000 to L.
.
B had NOT written any date on the cheque. But B did write amount Rs 1150,000
.
L admits that N had given undated cheque and L wrote date 1-feb-2015.
.
B had also written amount and date on the cheque as Rs 115,000/- and date as 1-feb-2015.
.
The cheque bounces. And B refuses to return the money. And say you are in Jury. Say you are in Jury. Then how many months of prison sentence would you impose? (0 --- means no prison sentence = acquittal )
.
My answer --- 0 months
.
===================
.
3. B = Borrower has borrowed Rs 100,000 from L = Lender on say 1-feb-2014 . And B had promised to return in writing on 1-feb-2015 i.e. 12 months later along with say 100% per year interest. Yes --- thats 100% interest, i.e. B has to pay twice the amount in just one year.
.
No cheque was given.

The search shows that B has no money to repay. And say you are in Jury. Say you are in Jury. Then how many months of prison sentence would you impose? (0 --- means no prison sentence = acquittal )
.
My answer --- 0 months
.
==============.
.
Please post your answers , if you like the question.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Mar 25 2015 03:31:41 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Power to crush the weak vs power to crush the strong "wrong" --- position and paidmedia-fame mostly gives first one and illusion of second.
.
Many suggest that good activists should join party such as CoBhAp = congress / bjp / aap , gain "power" and then implement their good agenda. And along with that, also gain massive coverage in paidmedia.
.
And the first step, namely joining CoBhAp means --- dump all good demands and proposals such as RTR, JurySys, wealth tax , gst abolition etc etc.
.
The suggestion IMO is wrong even in its best case scenario. Say a "good" activist dumps all good proposal, which is same as saying that he becomes as bad as any other bad activist or bad politician. Then he works increase following of CoBhAp-leaders , which is same as saying that he works to reduce following of other whatever small good senior activists may be out in field.
.
Now the owners of CoBhAp arent stupid. They can see thru the person. They will only let a junior come up if the junior gives them enough material by which the owners can bring him down if the leader in future betrays him.
.
But lets say against all odd, the "former" good activist manages to become tall leader in CoBhAp. Then possibility that he will bring good law-drafts is next to zero. Why?
.
One needs to differentiate between POSITION and POWER.
.
And for that, one needs to see difference is between POWER AGAINST WEAK and POWER AGAINST STRONG WRONG (or right).
.
A middle level police office will has immense power to crush a weak criminal or a weak good man. But he is absolutely powerless against a corrupt Commissioner or corrupt High Court Justice or corrupt Supreme Court Justice. But a few solo activists, who may be completely powerless against even a weak criminal, can be effective against such high profile corrupt / nepotic dignitaries. Why?
.
Thats because when a person gains "position", he becomes an elephant , but there are 100s of chains around it. And the chains are held by others. The elephants may be powerful than each entity separately, but much much weaker compared to the combined power of all the chain-holders. And so he can be very "powerful" in executing agenda of those who hold his chains. But for all other purposes, he is WEAKER than solo activist, because he has no freedom to even move an inch in that direction.
.
So all in all, so called positions get power ONLY in the direction wanted by those who hold the chains. In OTHER direction, position gets zero power. In fact, in other directions, position reduces freedom to act.
.
In today's context, what I mean is --- joining CoBhAp will ONLY decrease the ability to work against rising dominance of foreign elitemen , rising domination of Indian elitemen, worsening Maths / science education in govt schools , bring wealth tax etc.
.
So this ONLY proves that tall leaders who become tall via support of owners of paidmediamen are no good. That does NOT prove that small activists will be effective.
.
The way small activists are effective is that as more and more activists / voters know about their proposals, the tall-pseudo-activists are forced to speak their points. And that further increases coverage and that finally forces tall leaders to accept those points. Throughtout , tall-pseudo-activists and tall leaders would like to twist the points to make them ineffective, And thats why small-real-activists need to work hard.
.
example-1 : we small-activists at Right to Recall group publicized RTR-proposal in activists / citizens to extent that tall-pseudo-activists like Anna / ArKe were forced to atleast give lip service to this !!!
.
example-2 : we small-activists across net, not just RRG , but many more, started publicizing since 1995 that "freedom came to Bose, Bhagats et al, and Gandhi was a British partner and a Duratma". Now many psuedo-tall-activists like ArRoy, Katju are forced to echo this voice.
.
example-3 : we small-activists at RRG group publicized "decrease juvenile age to 16 years" , that some 20000 voters send orders to MP via SMS to decrease the age, This forced NaMo to issue "proposal" to decrease the age. The paidmedia twisted it as if NaMo has actually made such a law, and so SMS campaigned dispersed. But at least , now serious activists know that all NaMo did on this issue was just a drama.
.
example-4 : when Jindal got coal mine for Rs 108 / tonne , not even one SoMoKe-andhbhagat opposed it. Not even one paidmedia even focused on it. Only we at RRG started SMS campaign asking voters to order MPs via SMS to cancel the lease. And we threatened to give newspaper advertisement on this issue. So at least , a drama on this started. NaMo Govt cancelled the lease and now matter went to High Court.
.
So small activists can force psuedo-tall-activists like Anna , ArKe, ArRoy, Katju etc . That increases the coverage. And this forces tall-leaders to take some stand, and this further increases coverage. This is how goal can be achieved.

हम हिन्दुस्तानी..
.
हम हिन्दुस्तानी विश्वास करते है कि :

काला टीका बुरी नज़र से बचाता है
.
कोई ऊपर से निकल जाए तो कद नही बढ़ता
.
धूप में बरसात हो तो गीदड़ का ब्याह होता है
.
शाम के समय घर से बाहर नही जाते, न ही सीटी बजाया करते है
.
FDI जनित विकास से देश में खुशहाली आती है
.
चाय पीने से रंग काला हो जाता है
.
बिल्ली रास्ता काटे तो अशुभ होता है
.
हिचकी आये तो कोई याद कर रहा होता है
.
विदेश की कोई भी जगह भारत से बेहतर है
.
लोकल का मतलब घटिया होता है
.
कि अनशन करने से शक्ति मिलती है
.
कि हवा में कैंची चलाने से झगड़ा फैलता है
.
घोड़े की नाल और निम्बू मिर्ची लटकाने से बलाएँ दूर होती है
.
कि भारत में लोकतंत्र है
.
कि दायीं हथेली खुजाने से पैसे आते है, और बायीं से जाते है
.
.

हम विश्वास करते है इन सब बातों पर चाहे ये कितनी ही अजीब क्यों न हो..
हम इतनी अजीब चीजो को समझ लेते है, किन्तु यह साधारण बात क्यों नही समझ पाते कि :

कि भारत इसीलिए पिछड़ रहा है, क्योंकि हम अब तक बुरे कानूनों को ढो रहे है
.
कि ब्रिटेन और अमेरिका इसिलिये हम से आगे निकल गए क्योंकि उन देशो में राईट टू रिकाल और जूरी प्रथा जैसे क़ानून है
.
कि नेता नश्वर और क़ानून अमर होते है
.
कि अच्छे नेता नहीं, अच्छे क़ानून ही देश को मजबूत बना सकते है ।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Mar 25 2015 09:55:05 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Egyticians lost against Greeks and Romans even though Egyptians had an older civilization and were far far ahead of Romans in terms of Engineering etc. The reason was --- they wasted efforts in making pyramids, while Romans made roads, ships and weapons. But why Egyptians made such errors? Because Egypt was Monarchy , while Rome was mix of Oligarchy and Democracy.
.
Egyptians had an older civilization compared to Greece and Romans. And Egyptians were far far ahead of Romans in terms of Engineering , Maths, Science etc. The proof is pyramids. The pyramids are so mighty and complex construction that today's engineers cant figure out how to make such structure even with today's technology !!! And Pyramids arent just brick over bricks --- there are tunnels inside them and is whole structure isnt made carefully then tunnels will simply get crushed. Each stone weighs several tonnes taking stone to height is a task by itself.
.
Romans too were good at engineering, which is evident from roads , aqueducts etc they made. But when one compares structures Romans made with Egypticians made, the structures of Egytians are league above. So Egypticians were better in Engineering than Romans. But Romans defeated Egyptians, and before that, Alexender defeated Egyptians too. Why? Simple --- Egypticians wasted time, money and energy in making pyramids while Romans spent time, money and energy in making roads, aqueducts, postal network etc. Pyramids are magnificient but dont decrease inefficiencies of people in economy. So finally, citizens become productive in country with roads and aqueducts, then in country with just pyramids. And Romans also made better weapons, and so finally they defeated Egypticians.
.
But why did such good engineers decided NOT to make weapons and instead only make taller and taller pyramids?
.
Because Egypt was ruled by "tall leaders" aka Pharaoh who were "sons of Sun God Ra" !!! They had absolute command over everything. And so psycophancy was so high that highest officials should to queue up to touch feet !! Its another matter that sometimes their Vazier aka Prime Minister would murder a weak Pharaoh and appoint himself as Pharaoh !! But he too would be a "son of Sun God Ra" !!
.
While Rome started mixture of Democracy and Oligarchy, and even when it became Empire under Emperor, many Democratic procedures stayed. eg local magistrates were always elected, and many officials were appointed by Senators and not by Emperors. And many trials were decided by broad or narrow Juries. Even when Rome became "monarchy", it was not as centralized as Egypt.
.
Excessive centralization of powers in few hands leads centralization of power in one hand and then it leads to such notion that "King is more important that citizens" and so things like grave of king becomes more important that roads and making weapons. .
.
So yes, engineering is important. But the distribution of political power plays important role in which specific engineering marvels will be made and which will get lesser or zero priority. Extreme concentration of power would ensure that society makes too many pyramids , palaces , temples etc and not many roads, aqueduct, weapons etc. . And lesser conentration of power enables society to focus on latter set of goods. And that can make a difference of life and death.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Mar 26 2015 01:25:28 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

The so called paid-media , including CIA-book aka Facebook, are creations of sheer hard work. And those who complain that "media didnt cover my pet news" are unknowingly acting like "freeloaders". They unknowingly want benefits from others' hard work for free. .
.
Saying that "media didnt report our news" is same as saying "that restaurant didnt give ,me food for free" !! .
.
Solution : work, earn, and give newspaper advertisements for your causes.
.
===
.
From day one, we recallists have said that the "activists MUST pay for the newspaper advertisements to inform lakhs and crores of voters about important problems and solutions".
.
But almost all OTHER activists , unknowingly, want to be "freeloaders". They insist that the paidmedia should cover their news etc for free. And that may not alays happen. And so they cry that "paidmedia didnt cover our group / events / causes " etc.
.
Let me explain why people who want paidmedia to cover their events for free are , unknowingly, freeloaders i.e. they want benefit from others' labor for free.
.
The paidmedia isnt just money. It is money plus a lot of hard work. And money too came from hard work only.
.
The paidmediamen have to work hard to convince readers / viewers to read / watch their newspaper / TV-channel. And paidmediamen have to work 10 times harder to convince readers / viewers NOT to read / view any other newspaper / TV-channel or YT-video !!!
.
To make sure that readers read / watch their newspaper / TV-channel , they have to present a lot of entertainment across the newspaper / channel. And for that, they have to hire many entertainers of all types -- artists, musicians, makeup men, sports players etc . And it costs money to provide entertainment. I repeat --- it costs huge amount of money to provide entertainment in newspaper / TV-channels. And it also tales a lot pof hard work to administer them.
.
In addition , they have to cover interview of 10000s of small / large people every year so that a viewer sticks to them. The reason why paidmedia covers so many non-political events round the clock is because each such event attracts 100 to 10000 to 10000 to lakh to few lakhs of viewers depending on the event. And that increases readers' / viewers' loyalty as well as ensures that he doesnt read / view other newspapers / channel !!
.
Why do newspapers put 20-30 and sometimes 60 pages !!!? And why do TV-channels provide 24 hour shows and each channel-owning company starts more and more channels? It is to ensure that readers / viewers do NOT get time to see other newspapers and channels !!!
.
So paidmediamen work hard to ensure that citizens read / watch their newspapers / Tvchannels and not other newspapers and TV-channels.
.
And this is very expensive,
.
And its business --- they want to recover the costs plus profits.
.
Now let me give another example.
.
Say one = you has invested crores of rupees to make say a restaurant. Then how much free food you will give to people around one --- be rich or poor? At the end of the day, one made restaurant for profits. And so one's MOST activities will be for profits only.
.
Same applies for newspapers and TV-channels.
.
Saying that "media didnt report our news" is same as saying "that restaurant didnt give ,me food for free" !! Well, wake up and smell coffee. That restaurant does give food for free to anyone --- spare some special events. And that paidmediamen doesnt give free news to anyone, --- not even Congress , BJP or AAP; and not even to SoGa , NaMo or ArKe. So who are you to say that "resturant should give free food to me"? And who are you to ask that "paidmedia should cover my news for free"?
.
Solution I propose is that activists should work, earn, and then start their own newspapers OR give advertisements in existing newspapers. OR do both. And they should STOP expecting that resturant owner should give food for free. And should stop expecting that paidmedia should give media coverage for free.
..

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Mar 26 2015 01:52:26 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Will gohatya of old cow / bullocks reduce productivity young cows / bullocks? I think so. And here is why.
.
Here is a thought experiment.
.
Imagine a community of say 10000 persons. There are of all age group. .
.
But people in that community see a strange unexplained phenomenon --- that everyone who reaches age of 50 years suddenly disappears !! They never saw anyone above 50 years getting killled. But they did notice that everyone when he reaches age of 50 years suddenly disappears !!
.
In such case, almost every young person , even 20 years old, will feel threatened --- what will happen to me when I reach age of 50 years? IOW, all their lives , they will live in fear and the productivity will be low.
.
Instead, if each person sees every person around him live till old age, and see die a normal natural death, then there will only be natural fear of death, but not a fear that someone will make me disappear when I reach 50 years old.
.
IOW, lets say that a slave-owner decided to reduce costs by killing all slaves above 50 years who produced less than what it takes to keep them alive. Then immediate effect will be --- fear in all young slaves will also increase and their productivity will decrease.
.
======
.
Now lets apply this on cows / bullocks.
.
If cows / bullocks are completely dumb, then this wont apply. But thats not the case. Cows / bullocks are quite intelligent creatures..
.
So if old cows and bullocks are taken away, even silently under the veil of darkness, next day, the young cows and bullocks will notice that elder members have disappeaed. And this will increase fear in them and will reduce their productivity.
.
Now for better answers , one needs to compare cows / bullocks in areas where slaughter of old cows / bullocks is common with health of cows / bullocks where slaughter is not there. I do NOT suggest any experiment --- because I am against cow slaughter. But there are many countries where it is legal. So study of health of cows there can be made there, and compared with health of cows here.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Mar 26 2015 02:25:50 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Can anyone translate this in Hindi?
.
We request all to read
.
Hindustan Republican Party Manifesto , which was released by Mahatma Chandrashekhar Azad et al on 1-jan-1925
.
"CHAOS IS NECESSARY TO THE BIRTH OF A NEW star."' And the birth, and is passing through that inevitable chaos and agony. Indians shall play their destined role, when all calculations shall prove futile, when the wise and the mighty shall be bewildered by the simple and the week, when great empires shall crumble down and new nations shall arise and surprise humanity with the splendour and glory which shall be all its own.
This new power, which is shaking the world from its very depths, this new spirit which is working miracles behind the scene, is also manifesting itself in the young blood of India and is taking the shape of a movement which is despised and ignored by the wise and the learned, and is being described as the wild dreams of a few mad men. This remarkable movement is the revolutionary movement in young India. The revolutionary movement has unnerved the weak, has inspired the robust and healthy, and has confounded the worldly wise and the learned. This movement can never be crushed just as much as the coming of the spring can never be thwarted. It will never die out until it has fulfilled the mission for which it has taken its birth. Tyrants will oppress it, the faithless will taunt at it, and the confounded will denounce it, but thoughts and ideas can never be crushed by the sword, and the noble impulse that has taken birth in the very depths of out being can never be ignored, nor taunted.
This revolutionary movement is the manifestation of the new life that has taken birth in the Nation. To denounce this life is to denounce one's own understanding.
.
Twenty years of ruthless repression has not been able to crush it. Scathing denunciation by the renowned public leaders has not been able to arrest its steady growth. The movement stands mightier today than what it was before. The prospects of this revolutionary party were never so bright as they are today. This future is assured.
.
Let no Indian deny the existence of this revolutionary party in order to denounce the repressive measures of the foreign rulers. The foreigners have no right to rule over India and therefore they must be denounced and driven out. Not that they have committed any particular act of violence or crime. There are the natural consequences of a foreign rule. This foreign rule must be abolished. They have no justification to rule over India except the justification of sword and therefore the revolutionary party had taken to the sword. But the sword of the revolutionary party bears ideas at its edge.
.
The immediate object of the revolutionary party in the domain of politics is to establish a federal Republic of United State of India by an organized and armed revolution. The final constitution of this Republic shall be framed and declared at a time when the representatives of India shall have the power to carry out their decision. But the basic principles of this Republic will be universal sufferege and abolition of all system which make the exploitation of man by man possible, e.g. the railways and other means of transportation and communication, the mines and other kinds of very great industries such as the manufacture of steel and ships all these shall be nationalised.
.
-------------> In this Republic the electors shall have the right to recall their representatives, if so desired, otherwise the democracy shall become a mockery. <--------------
.
In this Republic, the legislature shall have the power to control the executives and replace them whenever necessity will arise.
.
The revolutionary party is not national but international in the sense that its ultimate object is to bring harmony in the world by respecting and guaranteeing the diverse interests of the different nations. It aims not at cooperation between the different nations and states and in this respect it follows the footsteps of great Indian Rishis of the glorious past and of Bolshevik Russia in the modern age. Good for humanity is no vain and empty word with the Indian revolutionaries. But the weak, the coward and the powerless can do no good either to themselves or to humanity.
With regard to the communal question, the revolutionary party contemplates to grant whatever rights the different communities ma demand, provided they do not clash with the interests o other communities and they lead ultimately to hearty and organic union in different communities in the near future.
.
In the domain of economic and social welfare the party will foster the spirit of cooperation on as large a scale as possible. Instead of private and unorganised business enterprises, the party prefers cooperative union.
In the spiritual domain the party aims at establishing the truth and preaching it that the world in not Maya, an illusion to be ignored and despised at, but that it is the manifestation of the one individual soul, the supreme source of all power, all knowledge and all beauty.
.
The revolutionary party has its own policy and its own programme. It cannot for obvious reasons divulge all its secrets. But when it will become quite sure that the Govt. happens to know more than our own people, then the public will also be informed of its plan and methods without any hesitation at will.
.
This revolutionary party pursues the policy of cooperation when possible and dissociation where necessary with the Congress and its different parties. But this party views all constitutional agitation in this country with contempt and ridicule. It is a mockery to say that India's salvation can be achieved through constitutional means, where no constitutions exists.
It is a self-deception to say that India's political liberty can be attained through peaceful and legitimate means. When the enemy is determined to break the peace at his own convenience, the fine phrase "legitimate" loses all its charm and significance when one pledges himself to maintain peace at all costs.
.
Our public leaders hesitate to speak in plain terms that India wants complete autonomy free from foreign control. They perhaps are ignorant of the fact that nations are born through the inspiration of great ideals. The spiritual ideal which hestitates to accept the spirit of complete autonomy can hardly be called spirititual, though it may seemingly appear the most unmistakable terms and to place before the nation an ideal worth the name.
.
The ideal before us is to serve humanity in an organized way. The ideal can never be realised by India so long as India remains British India. In order that India may realise her ideal she must have a separate and independent existence. This independence can never be achieved through peaceful and constitutional means. Even a child can understand that the laws that govern British India are not made by Indians, nor can they have any control over them. British India can never be transformed into a federal republic of the United State of India through the British laws and constitution. Young Indians, shake off your illusion, face realities with a stout heart, and do not avoid struggle, difficulties and sacrifices. The inevitable is to come. Do not be misguided any more. Peace and tranquility you cannot achieve by peaceful and legitimate means. The following memorable words of a great English author Mr. Robertson may serve to make the wise men of India wiser still:
.
"The movement and programme of reform was mainly the achievement of Irish and Protestant leaders, to whom British statement had revealed the fatal secret that England could be bullied but not argued into justice and generosity" (English Under Hanoverians, p. 197).
Indian public leaders are still ignorant of this fatal secret, or else they are foolishly wise to remain ignorants.
.
The wise men of India say that it is absurd to cherish the hope that India can be reconquered by force of force of arms, though they forget that it is equally or more absurd to believe that a handful of Englishmen have kept under subjugation by the force of arms, though they forget that it is equally or more absurd to believe that a handful of Englishmen have kept under subjugation by the force of arms one-fifth of the whole human race. Posterity may well doubt the authenticity of this fact that a handful of Englishmen even ruled over India for a century; it is so inconceivable.
A few words more about terrorism and anarchism. These two words are playing the most mischievous part in India today. They are being invariably misapplied whenever any reference to revolution arises to be made, because it is so very convenient to denounce the revolutionary under that name. The Indian revolutionaries are neither terrorists nor anarchists. They never aim at spreading anarchy in the land and therefore they can never properly be called anarchists. Terrorism is never their object and they cannot be called terrorists. They do not believe that terrorism alone can bring independence and they do not want terrorism for terrorism's sake although they may at times resort to this method as a very effective means of retaliation. The present Govt. exists simply because the Foreigners have successfully been able to terrorise the Indian people. The Indian people do not love their English masters, they do not want them to be here; but they do help the Britishers simply because they are terribly afraid of them and this very fear resists the Indians from extending their helping hands to the revolutionaries, not that they do not love them.
.
The official terrorism is surely to be met by counter-terrorism. A spirit of utter helplessness pervades every strata of our society and terrorism is an effective means of restoring the proper spirits in the society without which progress will be difficult. Moreover, the English masters and their hired lackeys can never be allowed to do whatever they like, uninterrupted, unmodested. Every possible difficulty and resistance must be thrown in their way. Terrorism has an international bearing also, because the ardent enemies of England are at once drawn towards Indian through terrorism and revolutionary demonstrations, and the revolutionary party has deliberately abstained itself from entering into this terroristic campaign at the present movement even at the greatest of provocations in the form in the form of outrages committed on their sisters and mothers by the agents of a foreign government, simply because the party is waiting to deliver the final blow. But when expediency will demand it, the party will unhesitatingly enter into a desperate campaign of terrorism, when the life of every official and individual who will be helping the foreign ruler in any way will be made intolerable, be he Indian or European, high or low. But even then the party will never forget that terrorism is not the object, and they will try incessantly to organize a band of selfless and devoted workers who will devote their best energies towards the political and social emancipation of their country.
.
They will always remember that the making of nations requires the self-sacrifice of thousand of obscure men and women who care more for the idea of their country than for their own comfort or interest, their own lives or the lives of those whom they love.
.

विश्व में मेरे देश का योगदान - शून्य !!!
.

कैसे हल होते गणित के सवाल जो हमनें न किया होता शून्य का आविष्कार ?
.
क्या होता दुनिया का हाल जो हमनें न दिया होता सत्य, अहिंसा और अपरिग्रह का विचार ?
.
कहाँ से आता खाने में स्वाद, जो न होते मसाले और दालचीनी लाजवाब ?
.
कौन जान सकता था कि, पौधों में भी है भावनावो का अहसास ?
.
क्या होता, जो न होता योग, न आयुर्वेद, न होता हमारा कामसूत्र ?
.
किसे सूझती सितार की मधुर झंकार ?
.
न होता काव्य, दर्शन, प्रेम का शिल्प यादगार
.
कौन सोचता शतरंज की चाल ?
.
कहाँ से आता पोलो का रोमांच ?
.
किसे सूझता ईश्वर का विचार ?
.
कौन नापता पृथ्वी की चाल ?
.
.

कौन जानता था कि सोने की चिड़िया कहलाने वाला तीसरी दुनिया का यह देश कहलायेगा एक गरीब और कमजोर राष्ट्र ?
.
किसने सोचा था कि हथियारों के अलावा सभी क्षेत्रों में प्रगति करने वाला यह देश विदेशी आक्रमणकारियों द्वारा लूटा जाएगा बार बार ?
.
किसे पता था अफगानिस्तान से फिलिपिन्स और कश्मीर से श्री लंका तक फैला यह देश खंड खंड होकर बिखरेगा ?
.
कब समझेंगे इस देश के वासी सेना का महत्त्व और करेंगे हथियारों की मांग ?
.
कब तक करेंगे कल्कि का इन्तिजार ?
.
उधार के हथियारों पर टिका यह देश कब तक बचेगा उनसे, जिनके पास है आधुनिक हथियारों के भण्डार ?
.
किसे पता है आने वाले वक़्त में ये देश फिर से होगा एक गुलाम देश ?

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Mar 27 2015 01:20:30 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Hi Naveen Singh ,
.
I now have over 4500 friends and most of them are politically active. I am answering over 20-30 questions per day. To save my time, I need you to follow the following SIMPLE instructions. Please read them, and please FOLLOW these instructions carefully to minimize my time. - Thanks
.
====
.
1. Our comments will be useful for other volunteers. So pls never delete a comment , unless lines of that comment are moved into other comment.
.
2. Pls combine CONSECUTIVE (only consecutive comments as follows)
.
2a. Pls click on edit comment-1
2b. Pls copy the content of comment-2 into comment-1
2c. Then pls delete the lower comment-2
.
3. Pls do NOT combine non-consecutive comments. PLEASE DO NOT COMBINE NON-CONSECUTIVE COMMENTS
.
4. To clarify what is consecutive comment and what is non-consecutive comment, let me give example. say
.
comment-1 is by you
comment-2 is by me
comment-3 is by you
comment-4 is by you
.
Then comment-3 and comment-4 are CONSECUTIVE comments and pls combine them
.
comment-1 and comment-3 are non-consecutive comments and so pls DO NOT combine them
.
5. Pls see my notes section on FB, and cntrl-F on SMS. There are notes on "citizens should order MPs via SMS". They are central to my campaign. Pls also download rahulmehta dot com slash 301.htm
.
6. Pls note that unlike other leaders, we NEVER ask activists to collect donations, hold our pictures, do naarebaazi etc. We only request activists to spread information about law-drafts, and we answers ALL questions for free. So I request ALL to putl comments and questions in PROPER textbook like Hindi or English, and NOT use SMS english, SMS abbreviation, and also not use informal writing style, as that makes reading time consuming and painful. Pls also avoid ALL CAPS posts.
.
7. If you have Q, pls first do ask Q to other volunteers. If you dont get satisfactory reply , then you are welcome to post it here. But before that, pls do ask Q to other volunteers.
.
8. Pls remember this thread's link by cntrl-D . In case you forget the link to this thread, please see "about me" -> "Full Info" on my page. There, you will see link to "two persons conversation threads" . Please search for your name there .
.
Thanx
.

Sat Mar 28 2015 05:43:06 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

International coal prices ( pls google on "international coal prices" ) = Rs 4000 per tonne to Rs 6000 /tonne (prices vary day to day). This is price at the post of source country
,
In India, same price can be obtained at the mine.
.
But most auction have fetched between Rs 800 / tonne and Rs 2000 / tonne. There are some outliers like Rs 3500 / tonne , but there are also some outliers like Rs 108 / tonne.
.
Why were bids so low compared to international prices?
.
(a) Small companies were not allowed to bit by making qualifying conditions cumbersome and also putting restrictions on capital the company must have.
.
(b) In many cases, restrictions were put that company must be power company or steel company !!!
.
(c) In many cases, restrictions were put that company must have capaibility and experience in mining.
.
(d) open auctions were NOT held ; e-auction were held which enables server owners to rigg bidding process and yet public cant see. If auction is made in public (like cricketers are auctions) , then manipulation still may happen, but whole world will see !!
.
Solution :-
.
1. Right to Recall coal minister --- so that we can get a coal minister who conductions auction in open and doesnt put useless conditions.
.
Please send following order to MP via SMS to get RTR over Coal Minister ---- " Dear MP , please print Right to Recall specific Minister law-draft in the Gazette so that we voters can replace Ministers in charge of dept , other than Defence , Home etc and dept such as Education, Chemical, Fertilizer etc . For full draft , pls see http://tinyurl.com/RtrMinister "
.
2. Create uniform coal market across India i.e. Railways should take coal from railway station X to railway station Y at neat flat rate IRRESPECTIVE of distance , i.e distance should pay minor role in frieght of coal. This necessary to have level field for coal consumers and promote TRUE competition between coal consumers
.
3. The bidding should be on cost of mining, and lowest cost giving contractor should be given mine
.
4. Coal buyers should be required to pay international price, and difference between (coal selling price - miners' costs) should be deposited in accounts of citizens DIRECTLY
.
5. Mining companies should NOT be owned by coal buyers --- partioning companies.
.
=====
.
And same logic should be applied for all mining.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Mar 28 2015 06:25:43 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Decreasing use of unaccounted cash and thus decreasing taxes on honest tax payers.
.
Accounted cash when buyer B pays seller S, S does declare it as his income, while buyer B may or may not declare it as his expense. A payment made via bank using cheque or credit card is always accounted because bank is witness. So seller S cant deny.
.
Now many persons' all income is all cheque. But often they withdraw cash, and give it to seller S, and S will not report it as income and not pay taxes. So govt is forced to raise taxes on honest tax payers. This is how unaccounted cash transfers increase burden in on honest tax payers.
.
So how can we allow cash payments, and also ensure that cash payments do get properly accounted? IMO, following system can work
.
1. buyer B will option to give his id to seller S , and S will put it on receipt or need not. When payment is done by card or cheque, buyer B's tax-id will automatically come on recipient,
.
2. say buyer B had paid Rs X
.
3. say seller S obtained Rs Y of revenue in whole FY = Financial Year and paid taxes of Rs T.
.
4. Then next year, buyer B will get tax credit for 50% * X * T / Y
.
5. If seller S doesnt report the id of B, then B cannot complain (the remedy for B is to use card or use trusted sellers).
.
IOW, say a person got salary of Rs 10 lakhs. He paid taxes of Rs 2.5 lakhs. And he spent Rs 3 lakhs using cash for check via various sellers. Say some sellers didnt report his expenditure with his id and some sellers did. Say Rs 2 lakhs of his expenditure was reported with his id to 100 sellers S1 to S100 , and expenses were X1 to X100. And say taxes they paid in that FY was T1 to T100., and their total incomes were Y1 to Y100.
.
Then next year, tax credit buyer B will get will be
.
50% * [ (X1 * T1 / Y1) + (X2 * T2 / Y2) + .... + (X100 * T100 / Y100)
.
To give an example in numbers, say buyer spent Rs 300000 with 4 sellers S1, S2, S3 and S3 and he bought goods worth Rs 50000 , Rs 50000, Rs 100000 and Rs 10000 from them.
.
Say S4 didnt report B's Ids. So B wont gain on S4's tax credits.
.
Say S1 made losses and paid no taxes. So buyer B wont gain anything from S1
.
Say S2 and S2 made total sale of 1 crore and 2 crore. And taxes they paid were Rs 5 lakhs and Rs 8 lakhs.
.
Then tax credit B will get from S1 = 50% * (5 lakhs / 1 crore ) * Rs 50000 = 50% * 5% * Rs 50000 = Rs 12500
.
And tax credit B will get from S1 = 50% * (5 lakhs / 1 crore ) * Rs 50000 = 50% * 5% * Rs 50000 = Rs 1250
.
And tax credit B will get from S2 = 50% * (8 lakhs / 2 crore ) * Rs 100000 = 50% * 4% * Rs 100000 = Rs 2000
.
So next year, buyer B will have to pay Rs 1250 + Rs 2000 = Rs 3250 less tax.
.
IOW, tax credit's incentive will make a buyer use card \ cheque as far as possible. This will reduce tax evasion and thus reduce excess tax on honest tax payer.
.
======
.
How US elitemen reduced unaccounted cash by .
.
(1) ensuing that a large number of people are into debt !! This forces then to use card. And so seller's income does get registered.
.
(2) by giving low interest loans to large corporations ; when a large corporations take amounts from 1000s or lakhs of customers, they take it via their employees such cash register clerks ; so if cash isnt registered then employees may rip-off , and so cash registration is streamlined
.
(3) rigging stock markets which enable large companies to get more capital
.
(4) jury trials on tax evasions.
.
Option (4) is IMO good. The other options are IMO unsustainable i.e. they improve tax collection but weaken smaller employees.So I dont support them.
.

मैं कहना यह चाहता हूँ कि भारत के 95% कार्यकर्ताओं ने कभी भी उन क्रान्तिकारियो की सुध नहीं ली जिन्हे आजादी के बाद अपना जीवन नारकीय अवस्था में बिताना पड़ा ।

.
लेकिन हम उन स्वत्रंता सेनानियों की दुर्दशा के लिए किन्हें जिम्मेदार ठहराएं, जिन्हें आजादी के बाद अपना जीवन बेरोजगारी के कारण भुखमरी में गुजारना पड़ा, क्योंकि जेल में भोगी गयी कठोर शारीरिक और मानसिक यातनाओ ने उन्हें अपाहिज बना दिया था ?
.

और सिर्फ दत्त ही नही, ऐसे हजारो क्रांतिकारी है, जिनके साथ हमने हद दर्जे की उपेक्षा की ।
.
कई हज़ार आजाद हिन्द फ़ौज के सैनिक अपंगता और भुखमरी की हालत में जीवन जीने को मजबूर थे ।

.

.

.

.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Mar 29 2015 14:08:56 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

The proposed WT = wealth law draft is to collect a wealth tax on land, construction, gold, shares, bonds, cash holdings etc . The wealth tax will be about (1% per year of value of (wealth minus basic wealth deductions) ) minus income tax paid, minus other taxes paid minus other contributions and minus wealth tax credit accumulated over past years).
.
Dear citizens of India ,
.
My claim is that the proposed WT-MITMSC = Wealth Tax Minus Income Tax paid Minus Several Credits , will reduce the land prices, and will also reduce speculation in share markets. Reduction in land prices will reduce the amount a person has to spend in buying house or rents, and will increase his incomes minus rent. Reduction in land price will increase business and factories and will reduce unemployment. And it will increase income tax. And the reduction in land price will also reduce the problem of land acquisition.

=====

Pls take a look at the proposed law-draft. For explanation, you can search on FB for Right to Recall Group activists in your city. You can call \ meet them and they will explain the clauses to you. After explanations , if you support this law-draft, then please send following order to MP via SMS.
.
=========== start of order to be sent to MP via SMS ==========
.
Dear MP , I order you to print the law-draft mentioned in http://tinyurl.com/WealthTaxIndia
.
======= end of sms text ====
.
Dear MP,
.
If you have received the link to this law-draft then it is an order from YOUR voter , and NOT this author, to print the following law-draft in Gazette
.
===== start of the law-draft ====
.
section-1 : Basic definitions
.
(1.1) The word citizen in this draft would mean a registered adult voter
.
(1.2) The word land in this draft will include all plots, agricultural land and flats, offices, buildings, ownership over a plot created by owning a consutruction. And the word flat in this draft will include all construction --- apartments , bungalows , offices, buildings, warehouses, industrial shades and all other constructions.
.
section-2 : Main officers and their staff , offices
.
(2.1) ( instruction to PM )
.
The PM will appoint an officer titled as NWTO = National Wealth Tax Officer to collect wealth tax. The citizens of India may replace him using Right to Recall NWTO clauses listed in RTR-NWTO section
.
(2.2) ( instruction to NWTO . PM , MPs , citizens )
.
NWTO will prepare guidelines and give requirements of funds for running his offices across India. The guidelines will come into effect after PM prints them in Gazette or after MPs pass them as legislation or after citizens approve them using clauses given in the TCP-section of this draft
.
(2.3) ( instruction to NWTO )
.
NWTO will recruit existing officers from Central Govt or any State Govt , after approval of their respective heads. NWTO will appoint one SWTO = State Wealth Tax Officer , one DWTO = District Wealth Tax Officer per District, and appoint one TWTO = Tahsil Wealth Tax Officer per Tahsil. They will all work under Central Govt under NWTO .
.
section-3 : Guidelines to make wealth tax rules and change wealth tax rules
.
(3.1) Parliament may amend this wealth tax laws as necessary. PM too may make rules and amend the rules under this law using GNs as needed.
.
(3.2) NWTO may issue circulars to calculate and collect wealth tax as needed.
.
(3.3) When a circular is issued by NWTO, it will be sent to all DWTO across India who will summon 12 Jurors at random. DWTO will explain the circular to the Jurors and Jurors may ask any CA or any person to present his views on the circular. And if majority of Jurors across India oppose the circular, then NWTO may or need not resign , and may or need not cancel the circular. His decision will be final.
,
(3.4) The citizens may issue a circular using TCP-clauses given in TCP-section of this draft.
.
section-4 : Filing wealth tax return
.
(4.1) Each wealth owner , except Indian citizens who have wealth below basic deductions. All foreign entities, all companies and all trust must file wealth tax returns.
.
(4.2) The wealth tax return must be filed within 210 days after closing of financial year, and must be filed within 30 days after income tax return is filed.
.
(4.3) The tax payer can use his PAN-ID or Adhar-ID to file his wealth disclosure. If he doesn’t have any of the two IDs, then he must obtain one ID within 3 months after this law is printed in Gazette.
.
(4.4) For HUF, the Karta can file separate return for HUF in which HUF will get no basic deductions, or can add HUF’s wealths with his wealth, and file a return.
.
section-5 : Basic deduction from wealth tax
.
This section has basic deduction of human entities below age of 60 years. .
.
(5.1) The basic deductions for all non-person entities , such as companies, trusts, HUFs etc will be zero. The basic deductions for foreigner persons will be zero.
.
(5.2) The basic deductions for persons above age of 60 years will be twice than basic deductions listed below. And for those above age of 80 years will be 4 times the basic deductions. The basic deductions of widows and widowers will be also twice. However, for widows or widowers of above age of 60 years, it will be only 2 times and not four times; and for widows or widowers of above age of 80 years, it will be only 4 times and not eight times.
,
(5.3) Unused deductions can be transferred to a family member. For definition and eligibility of family members, please see later sections

(5.4) the non-agricultural plots - 25 sqmt per person, and unused deductions can be transferred to a family member .
.
(5.5) construction above 50 sqmt per person, and unused deduction can be transferred to a family member.
.
(5.6) agricultural land below 2 acres per family member ; and unused deduction can be transferred to a family member
.
(5.7) gold \ silver \ diamonds \ precious stones \ jewelry worth below Rs 50000 per male person and below Rs 2.5 lakh per female person ; and unused deduction can be transferred head of family.
.
(5.8) vehicles worth below Rs 1 lakh per person ; and unused deduction can be transferred to a family member.
.
(5.9) all shares \ bonds \ debentures owned in companies , in which companies are paying wealth tax on their shares \ bonds \ debentures , will be exempt from share owner
.
(5.10) shares \ bonds \ debentures worth below Rs 100,000 , owned in companies , in which companies are paying wealth tax on their shares \ bonds \ debentures ; this deduction isn’t transferable
.
(5.11) cash-in-hand below Rs 1 lakh per family member ; ; and unused deduction can be transferred to a family member.
.
(5.12) furniture, electrical items, electronic items, hardware, painting, art forms below Rs 3 lakhs per family member; ; and unused deduction can be transferred to a family member
.
(5.13) wealth outside India is not exempt unless person is returning NRI in which case wealth of Rs 10 lakhs per person will be exempt ; and unused deduction can be transferred to family member
.
(5.14) intellectual property such software, brandname, patents, copyrights, will be exempt from wealth tax for person as well as non-person entities
.
section-6 : WTC = Wealth tax credits for previous years
.
Several taxes and other payments made in past 4 years will become wealth tax credit.
.
(6.1) WTC will be calculated for each financial year separately. It will be labeled as WTC-AY-YYYY-(YYYY+1) i.e. WTC for Financial Year starting 1-apr-YYYY to 31-mar-(YYYY+1).
.
(6.2) WTC will be calculated for 4 years before this wealth tax law starts.
.
{ Explanation :
.
FY = Financial Year , AY = Assessment Year.
.
Say wealth tax starts from FY 1-apr-2016 to 31-mar-2017 i.e. AY-2017-2018. and onwards.
.
Then WTC will be calculated for four previous years
.
WTC-AY-2013-2014 : wealth tax credit generated out of taxes paid for FY 1-apr-2012 to 31-mar-2013 i..e AY 1-apr-2013 to 31-mar-2014
.
WTC-AY-2014-2015 : wealth tax credit generated out of taxes paid for FY 1-apr-2013 to 31-mar-2014 , AY 1-apr-2014 to 31-mar-2015
.
WTC-AY-2015-2016 : wealth tax credit generated out of taxes paid for FY 1-apr-2014 to 31-mar-2015 , AY 1-apr-2015 to 31-mar-2016
.
WTC-AY-2016-2017 : wealth tax credit generated out of taxes paid for FY 1-apr-2015 to 31-mar-2016 , AY 1-apr-2016 to 31-mar-2017
}
..
(6.3) pro-rata income tax paid on non-salary income in an AY will be WTC for that AY
.
(6.4) pro-rata 50% income tax paid on salary income will be WTC
.
{ Explanation : if salary income is say Rs X and non salary income is say Rs Y and total tax paid was Rs T, then of tax from salary income will be Rs T * X / (X + Y) and tax paid from non-salary income will be Rs T * Y / (X + Y)
}
.
(6.5) wealth tax \ service tax \ excise \ vat \ octroi \ cst \ local body taxes \ pay roll tax\ professional tax\ municipal property tax and all taxes paid to central govt (except customs) , state govt or local govt in that AY as stated in the return will become WTC for that AY. Customs duty paid will not be counted towards WTC.
.
(6.6) for every employee , (0% of salary below Rs 2 lakhs, 5% of salary between Rs 2 lakhs and Rs 5 lakhs, 10% of salary between Rs 5 lakhs and Rs 10 lakhs ; and 15% of salary over Rs 10 lakhs) will be added to WTC
.
{ Explanation :
Say an employer had 3 employees and salaries paid in an AY were Rs 150,000/- , Rs 260,000/-, Rs 600,000/- and Rs 1200,000/- . The WTC from employee’s salaries will be Rs 0, Rs 3000 , Rs 25000 , Rs 95000/- respectively . Total WTC from employees’ salary will be sum of these four numbers.
}
.

(6.7) for every employer, 15% of the employer side PF paid by the employer will be added to WTC of employer
.
(6.8) The penalties on unpaid or late-paid taxes will be fully included. Only 50% of the interest paid due to non-payment or late-payment will be included.
.
(6.9) WTC will not consider actual date of payment but only the amount due in each FY or corresponding AY
.
(6.10) To claim and correct WTC of a year, a person will have at most 2 years after ending of that AY. For first 4 years’ WTC, it will be till 3 years after the wealth tax law implementation starts.
.
(6.11) In case of dispute on WTC amount, the govt records will be deemed final in case of discrepancy. The Jurors may revise the amount after a trial
.
section-7 : Wealth tax credits for subsequent years
.
For the years after passing the law, the Wealth Tax Credits for an AY consist of following
.
(7.1) income tax paid on non-salary income
.
(7.2) 50% of income tax on salary income
.
(7.3) all other taxes due to central govt (except customs), state govt and local govt , such as in that AY ; penalties and interest paid, and fines paid on taxes will not be included
.
(7.4) 15% of salaries paid by payee a/c cheque , or cash , if employee enters cash salary his cashbook maintained at the bank or mentions in his income tax return
.
(7.5) 15% of PF paid by the employer
.
(7.6) number of service takers multiplied by Rs 500
.
section-8 : Calculation of wealth tax
.
(8.1) LC = Land and construction will be valued at circle rate or purchase price, which ever is higher. There will be no depreciation on land and depreciation on construction will be 5% a year and indexation increase would apply.
.
(8.2) M = Machinery , vehicles, hardware will be valued at purchase price. and 10% annual depreciation and no indexation will apply. Furniture will be valued at purchase price and 5% annual depreciation and indexation will apply
.
(8.3) GS = Gold, silver, metals, diamonds and precious stone will be valued at annual average market price
.
(8.4) SP = (a) If company is paying wealth tax on shares, then taxable share price will be average market capitalization during the year minus value of land, construction, precious metals gold, precious stones (b) if owner is paying wealth tax then it will be average share price across the year (c) either company or owner but not both will pay wealth tax on share
.
(8.5) In each case, applicable basic deductions can be subtracted.
.
(8.6) Wealth tax payable will be 1% of (LC + MF + GS + SP ) minus applicable basic deductions.
.
(8.7) If wealth tax is negative then it will become wealth tax credit for that AY
.
(8.8) if wealth tax payable for that year is positive, then oldest WTC will be used to lower it. And when all WTC are exhausted , the tax payer will be required to pay the wealth tax.
.
(8.9) In case of inability to pay, the tax payer can request locking of his property, and in that case interest of 9% per year will apply. However, the value of property must be at least twice the outstanding tax
.
section-6 : Eligibility to become family member
.
(6.1) For the purpose of wealth tax, an individual can register himself as solitaire i.e. alone or part of family. which ever suits him best.
.
(6.2) One person cannot be member of two families. Persons registered as solitaire cant be member of any family. Non-person entity or foreigner cant be member of family for wealth tax purposes.
.
(6.3) A family cannot have more than 12 members. The family member must not have wealth above Rs 25 crore , not counting land and constructions.
.
(6.4) Family will consist of Head of the family, who can be male or female above 18 years of age.
.
(6.5) The spouses of Head can become member of the family.
.
(6.6) The children can become member of the family
.
(6.7) The parents and parents-in-law can become member of the family
.
(6.8) Children of son / daughter can become member of family , but only if son / daughter is also member of the family.
.
(6.9) The children of grand children cannot become member of the family
.
(6.10) Unmarried or divorced sister of the Head can be member of family. Brother below 18 years can become member of the family.

(6.11) Children of brother or sister cannot become member of family
.
(6.12) No one except above can become member of family
.
(6.13) If a person has more than 4 children, then only 4 can be part of family for wealth tax purposes.
.
(6.14) If a person wants to form family for wealth tax purpose, then he will need to register his family members by personally appearing at Patwari = Talati = Village Officer. And the members too will have too appear at office of any Patwari in India and acknowledge it. For minors, permission of both parents will be needed.
.
(6.15) The family members can transfer their basic deductions amongst themselves
.
(6;.15) The family members cannot transfer their WTC to anyone
.
section-Jury-Trial : Jury Trial for wealth tax disputes
.
(J.1) ( instruction to NWTO , all tax payers)
.
Wherever there is dispute between NWTO or his officer and tax payer, NWTO or his officers will form a CCA = Committee of Chartered Accountants and form a Jury to decide the case. This section gives rules of formation of CCA and the Jury.
.
(J.2) ( instruction to NWTO )
.
NWTO will appoint a Jury Administrator for each District called as DJA = District Jury Administrator, and one Jury Administrator for each State called as SJA = State Jury Administrator, and one Jury Administrator for India called as NJA = National Jury Administrator
.
(J.3) ( instruction to NJA , SJA , DJA, all WT officers , all tax payers)

(J.3.1) A tax dispute between a tax payer and tax officer will be filed in district in which tax payer is voter of or is resident of or has registered offices, in that order. If the tax payer or tax officer wants to change the venue to a district in same state, then may get order from SJA or State High Court. And if either of the two wish to change venue to a district in India outside the State , then he may get order from NJA or Supreme Court.
.
(J.3.2) For every tax dispute in district, the DJA = District Jury Administrator will randomly select 3 to 10 CAs from a list of CAs who have agreed to provide assistance in cases. The CAs selected must be between the age of 30 years and 55 years, and must have 5 years of experience as CA.
.
(J.2.3) DJA will randomly select voters from voter list of India between age of 30 and 55 years. The person must not have appeared in any Jury in past 10 years and must not have faced any conviction in past.
.
(J.3.3) Number of Jurors – The number of Jurors will be at least 12 and at most 1500. The Jurors . number Jurors will be 12, if the wealth tax evasion as claimed by WTO = Wealth Tax Officer is below Rs 10 lakh. And there will 1 extra Jurors for every Rs 10 lakhs of wealth tax evasion as claimed by WTO. The highest size will be 1500 Jurors
.
(J.3.4) Location of Jurors --- The Jurors will be selected from Districts whose District Courts are linked via video conferencing to districts where trial is being run. In the district isn’t connected with any district via video conferencing, then all Jurors will be from District where the case is filed.
.
(J.3.5) Disputes of valuation of wealth – if the dispute is only over valuation of land, then dispute will resolved by the Jurors in that district. The number of Jurors will be 12 + plus 1 Juror per Rs 10 lakh of difference in valuation , with maximum of 1500 Jurors. However , DWTO, SWTO or NWTO may ask for change of venue to decide the valuation of the land.
.
(J.3.6) Appeal against judgment of of District Jurors can be filed with Jurors or Judges in State High Court and later with Jurors or Judges in Supreme Court as decided by other prevailing laws
.
section-RTR-NWTO : Right to Recall NWTO
.
(RTR.1) ( instruction to Cabinet Secretary , Union Govt or officer he deputes )
.
If any citizen of India wishes to become NWTO , and he appears in person or via a lawyer with affidavit before the Cabinet Secretary, then the Secretary would accept his candidacy for NWTO after taking filing fee same as deposit amount for MP election.
.
(RTR 2) (instruction to Talati , or Talati’s Clerks)
.
If a citizen of that district comes in person to Talati’s office, pays Rs 3 fee , and approves at most five persons for the NWTO position, then the Talati would enter his approvals in the computer and would him a receipt with his voter-id#, date/time and the persons he approved.
.
(RTR.3) ( instruction to Talati )
.
The Talati will put the preferences of the citizen on PM's website with citizen’s voter-ID number and his preferences
.
(RTR.4) (instruction to Talati)
.
If a the citizen comes to cancel his Approvals, the Talati will cancel one of more of his approvals without any fee
.

(RTR.5) (instruction to Cabinet Secretary)
.
On every 5th of month, the CS may publish Approval counts for each candidate as on last date of the previous month.
.
(RTR.6) (instruction to PM )
.
If a candidate gets approval of over 51% of ALL registered citizen-voters (ALL, not just those who have filed their approval) in a district, then PM may or need not expel the existing NWTO and may or need not appoint the person with highest approval count as NWTO . The decision of PM will be final
.
section-TCP : TCP-clauses
.
(TCP.1) ( instruction to District Collector )
.
if any citizen wants a change in this law-draft, he may submit an affidavit at Collector’s office and Collector or his clerk will post the affidavit on the website of Prime Minister for a fee of Rs 20/- per page.
.
(TCP.2) (instruction to Talati or Patwari)
.
If any citizens want to register his opposition to this law-draft or any section or wants to register YES-NO to any affidavit submitted in above clause, and he comes to Talati’s office with voter-ID and pays Rs 3 fee, Talati will enter YES/NO and give him a receipt. The YES-NO will be posted on the website of the Prime Minister.
..
(TCP.3) (instruction to Talati or Patwari)
.
If any citizens want to register his opposition to this law-draft or any section or wants to register YES-NO to any affidavit submitted in above clause, and he comes to Talati’s office with voter-ID and pays Rs 3 fee, Talati will enter YES/NO and give him a receipt. The YES-NO will be posted on the website of the Prime Minister.
.

.

.
2. Activists can ask voters to send URL / URI to MP via SMS
.
3. Activists can ask voters to send another URL / URI to MP via SMS , which tells MP to setup SMS server and send ack# to citizen's SMS, and put URL ---- Count information on MP's website
.
4. Activist can ask net-enabled voters to put a comment in the FB status which has the draft.
.
5. Activist can ask net-enabled voters to create an FB status on their wall , which has text of the order they sent to MP via SMS , a JPG image, and URL to their comment in post in (4).
.
6. Activist can ask net-enabled voters to create an FB notes , which has list of all SMS they have sent, and URL to their comment in post in (4) as well as URL to (5).
.
This will make it possible activist to verify how many voters have sent SMS to MP --- they can look at number of comments in FB-status containing the draft in (1)
.
They can verify if an FB user has sent SMS or not. How? See the profile of that FB user. See his notes on his wall which has list of all SMS he sent. In that . In that, search for the URL given in (1). And see the link to comment he posted in (1).
.

.
A torrent based voting system would need each net-enabled voter to create a digital signature. And this scheme works as follows
.
1. A net-enabled voter will need to create digital signature and publish the public key
.
2. Say draft is put on torrent and SHA1 hash of that torrent is Sha1Draft . It will be 48 digit long alpha-numeric string.
.
3. The net enabled voter can create a string called "Sha1Draft-yes-date-time" and put the digest using private key portion of his digital signature.
.
4. The net enabled voter can put "Sha1Draft-yes-date-time" string along with the digest as a torrent. The digest is the proof that he digitally signed the string.
.
5. There can be several websites, which keep torrents from crores of voters or voters of a particular area like assembly segment , parliament segment etc.
.
Now sending verified digital signature of all 10 crore net-enabled voters to all 10 crore net-enabled voters is possible. And it is possible even if number of voters grow to 900 crore i.e. world population after 30 years !! It does require a human to to human network of activist where 1 out of 1000 person is activist and they communicate face to face once a week for 5 minutes a week.

Mon Mar 30 2015 13:05:19 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Green cap Red cap booth level meetings --- Green cap means speaker can speak , red cap means speaker should stop speaking
.
IMO, activists should work to hold green cap red cap meetings at their booth level. The caps dont have any affiliation with party etc. They are just like traffic lights.
.
1. say meeting of 1 , 2 or 3 (not more) booths are called . Booth is list of 500-800 voters in a voter list.
.
2. The attendees will be divided into 4 separate groups.
.
(1a) male voters of those booths and spouses of female voters in those booths
.
(1b) female voters of those booths and spouses of male voters of those booths.
.
(1c) male attendee who are NOT voters in those booths nor spouses of any female voters in those booths.
.
(1d) female male attendee who are NOT voters in those booths nor spouses of any male voter in those booths.
.
2. Each person will have to buy red cap and green cap or he may bring his own.
.
3. The organizers will need voter list to ensure that everyone in group (1a) and (1b) are voters or spouses of voters.
.
4. The organizer will use dices to select a random number between 1 and number of voters in that booth. And voter selected will give name of 1 speaker, Those who want that speaker to speak will immediately wear green cap and those who dont will wear red cap.Those who dont want to vote will wear no cap.
.
5. Those who have worn green cap will move on right and stand in queue of 10. And those who have worn red will come on left and stand in queue of 10. And thus counting will become fast.
.
6. Like that 5 voters will be chosen at random and each will suggest one speaker. Ad the speaker who gets highest greens will speak
.
7. Those who want speaker to end will wear red cap and those who wish that he should continue will wear green cap. And when red outnumber green, the speaker will have to end the speech. The speaker can suggest a name when he ends his speech.
.
8. So there are 4 speakers on whom red-green count is known. And one more was suggested by speaker. red-green count will be taken on him,
,
9. The one with green count will speak.
.
There are several variations. But what it means is that speaker will have to end when audience wants to hear someone else. How to select that "someone" else? Again, red-green count can be used.
.
The count of ONLY voters in the booths will be counted and NOT others who are attending (WHY?). The others who are attending may wear red-green cap. But they will NOT be counted in deciding if speaker should speak or end.
.
So control remains with audience on whom audience wants to speak and whom audience wants to shut up.
.
If control remains with audience, then speakers will restrain himself from wasting audience's time.
.
=========================
.
Splitting of sabha
.
If certain voters think that other voters are ganging up or acting unfairly, then they can always call a meeting amongst themselves at later time and date.
.
======================
.
Later , a few variations can be made to decide "who should speak" via audience.
.
I will soon start such red-green-cap meetings in ahmedabad. lets see how it goes.

RSS-apex claims that BJP-MPs may be corrupt , but their 2500 Parcharaks are large non-corrupt. So why didnt NaMo give tickets to 400 Pracharaks out of those 2500 Pracharaks?
.
And what sort of people are they, that they dont want to contest elections even it that is dire need of nation?
.
BJP had fielded 400 candidates in LS-2014 elections and some reaming 150 were fielded by NDA allies. NaMo / Bhagwat had power to give ticket ONLY to RSS-pracharaks if they wanted. But less than 10-20 pracharaks got tickets. And rest of the 380 tickets went to
.
The reason why NaMo / Bhagwat didnt give tickets to 400 Pracharaks is simple
.
1. Out of 400 , some 290-300 would have won, and within 3-4 months, over 80% would have become corrupt.
.
2. And that would have been end of RSS's prestige.and lakhs of RSS-volunteers will leave RSS and start alternate political alternatives and BJP's votes will move to new alternatives RSS-workers create.
.
RSS-apex retains influence in its members by claiming that "see, it is those BJP MPs who are defunct ; we RSS-apex and RSS-middle are all 100% non-corrupt and INCORRUPTIBLE !!". It is fact that 2500 RSS-pracharaks are non-corrupt because they dont have power. Well, even Laloo was non-corrupt when he didnt have power !! But the illusion they give is is "RSS-pracharaks are all INCORRUPTIBLE and when they get power Ram Rajya will come in India !!". And this hope is used to keep lakhs of members glued to RSS-apex and NOT start any political alternative.
.
And to keep their middle non-corrupt, they need to keep their middle away from political power !!
.
All in all, RSS-apex and BJP-apex has convinced them NOT to look beyond BJP , because one day 100s of RSS-pracharaks will become BJP MPs and bring Ram Rajya in India !!!
.
But fact that they didnt give MP tickets to 400 RSS-pracharaks who are INCORRUPTIBLE shows that it is something else.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Mar 30 2015 16:13:51 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Why PSUs , Army , DRDO etc dont take campus interview at IITs and other apex collges etc?
.
Supreme Court has instructed all PSUs that they must recruit ALL staff after giving newspaper advt and taking exams or interviews of all those who apply.
.
The judgment has some merit that PSUs must treat every candidate equally.
.
What DRDO etc can do is take a mock competitive tests inside IIT etc and give cash rewards for those who pass. And based on the results of the students, they can tell students their chances of getting recruited in DRDO if they were to apply in the real test. And DRDO etc can also announce high rewards for all those who come in say top 200 in their actual tests, whether they join or not. And DRDO should also give advertisements in IIT's internal student's magazines etc when tests are due.
.
And this applies for not just IITs , but a large number of good colleges, where almost every student gets absorbed in campus interview . Here in employer *will* have to take efforts to reach to the students.
.
This will increase interest of students of apex colleges in all PSUs.
.

.

.
RSS-apex was heavily aided by the then kings , such as Scindhia of Gwalior !! Why would British agents like Scindhia aid RSS-apex ? Because Hindi Mahasabha was asking youth to become political. And British needed someone to convince youth to focus on character building etc etc and NOT become political !! Duratma Gandhi and Jawaharlal Ghazi were running Ashrams and Seval Dal to convince youth to stay away from politics !!! But they werent able to impress Hinduvaadi youth. British needed "Hinduvaadi" leaders to tell youth to stay away from politics !! And so British ask schindhia etc to aid RSS-apex.
.
Today, also, RSS-apex asks its volunteers to stay away form politics. This stops volunteers from working on a REAL nationalist political alternative in India. And when election come, RSS-apex will ask all workers to work for BJP by showing them threat "see, if you dont support BJP then Congress will win and will ruin Hindus !!!!". So between elections, RSS-apex keeps volunteers busy with morning walks and blood bank to drain away their energy, so that they cant form any political alternative. And just 3 months before election, RSS-apex will tell the ovious that "BJP is only alternative to congress !!"
.
All in all, RSS-apex now gives stability and votes to BJP and nothing more. The downside is that billions of activists hours , which should be used ONLY to create TRUE nationalist political alternatives, get wasted away,.
.
Solution?
.
Solution is that I ask ALL RSS-volunteers to discuss law-drafts needed to reduce corruption , nepotism in judiciary , use wealth tax instead of gst, etc . And you will see the picture.

.

इसके प्रावधान के अनुसार :

1. यदि SP के सम्मुख कोई बयान दर्ज किया जाता है तो उस बयान को कोर्ट में बतौर सबूत माना जाएगा ।

.

राजनीति - वह निति जिससे राज काज किया जाएगा ।
.
राजनितिक - वह व्यक्ति जो ऐसी नीति बनाएगा ।
.
अच्छा राजनितिक - अच्छी नीति बनाने वाला ।
.
बुरा राजनितिक - बुरी नीति बनाने वाला ।
.
.
राजनीति चमकदार भासण और लफ्फाजी नहीं है । जनता को सरकार का आकलन उसकी नीतियों अर्थात बनाए गए कानूनों से करना चाहिए ।
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Mar 31 2015 03:31:47 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Yatha karyakarta , tatha niyam
.
Yatha niyam , tatha raja , tatha praja.
.
Means "nature of activists will decide nature of law-drafts in a country. And nature of law-drafts will decide nature of both people and rulers".
.
IOW, both statements that "yatha raja , tatha praja" and "yatha praja , tatha raja" are incorrect. They both impact each other --- but much much less than the impact law-drafts cause on both.
.
And law-drafts come not due to efforts of rulers or career intellectuals or "career activists" or citizens at large. Rulers, career intellectuals, career activists and citizens on their own ONLY bring minor changes in what ever law-drafts that already exist. The new law-drafts come MAINLY due to efforts of "non career" activists, provided these "non career" activists work to bring the law-drafts.
.
The activist also comes from population at large, but difference is that he is willing to put a few hours a week of his time, his own money etc in something with no expectation. .
.
The activists in 950 AD and later in 110 AD in UK worked to bring JurySys in UK. This brought many other changes , such as direct election of London School Board members in 1840 and many more, and strengthened UK to a point that they captured half the world. USA went several steps ahead and printed law-drafts for elected officials such as police chiefs / judges / public prosecutors , right to recall , stronger jury system , referendum etc and became so strong that it controls almost whole world (including India).
.
So solution to mess in India I propose is ---- "true activists" i.e. "non career activists" should focus on law-drafts , publciize the law-drafts and ask citizens to oppose all leaders who refuse to support these law-drafts. As the leaders weaken, they will run to bring this very law-drafts they opposed and also run to claim credit for bringing them. OR , their sponsors will appoint new career leaders who will claim that they brought these law-drafts and NOT the activists !!! But if activists publicize the ;law-drafts, then they can force leaders and their sponsors to print those law-drafts in Gazette against their will.
